**THE MAN WHO TRADES ON THE OPEN HAND BOARD.** Chapter 0340, and in no other of these ten. **He is given the trade and the board and NO AGE, deliberately, and the reason is a real discrepancy in the plan layer: `outline/volume-07.md` says a man of about thirty-seven, the prose of Volumes 02 to 06 says a man of about thirty-nine at eighteen named chapters, and Chapter 0309 of this volume says a man of about thirty-seven. They are the same man and a batch may not decide which figure is right.** Second handle: **a board, with nineteen rates on it and no names, carried face up because a man who trades on a board has never once carried one with the writing on the underside where the rain can get at it.** He has been asked to be added by name and refused in four seconds four times in four years, and every one of those times a woman who is on nothing and is named on nothing did the refusing. **What is on a page in Chapter 0340 and on no page before it: he refused a woman who is on nothing and is named on nothing at the back of a market, in nineteen words, about a hundred days into this flood, and has never said so to anybody; and about as long afterwards a man asked him to put his own name on that board in about the ninth year of this flood and he wrote it on in his own hand, and the man came and told the woman her name was not going on, and he took it off the next morning with his own cloth. About four hundred people in this city can see the place on the board where the name was. THE TWO DATES ARE SETTLED AND A LATER CHAPTER MAY NOT PUT THE REFUSAL OF THE WOMAN AFTER THE NAME WENT ON, because Chapter 0113 and Chapter 0123 are the page for the refusal and `outline/volume-07.md` §17.7 is the page for the name.** He refused the volume's one panel before a sentence was finished and the refusal is what made him say it.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY WITH A HANDCART AND A LENGTH OF ROPE.** Chapter 0340, and in no others of these ten. A return; he is on a page in Chapters 0300 and 0304 of this volume. **His rope has been used and is not new, and it is not the man of about fifty-seven's rope, which is new and has never been used, and the two are on a page separately and must stay separate.** He was told no at a gate and said he would come back about nine days from a morning, and it has been a good deal more than nine days, and he came back, **and he came into a room rather than to a gate, and he asked one question at about the fourth hour of the afternoon, which is whether a person can be put back, and nobody in that room answered him, and the wall does not answer it either.** He is the last image of the volume in Chapter 0348 and nobody has sent him.

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL, THE GIRL OF SEVENTEEN, THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY AT THE SHUT GATE, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT WITH THE BARROW, THE KEEPER OF THE REDROOT GATE, HETTIS ROKE, THE MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-ONE IN A VERY GOOD COAT, THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE, THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-THREE, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR AT THE WHARF WITH THE TALLY, AND THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-EIGHT** are in none of these ten chapters. **The woman of about twenty-eight is a return and appears once, in Chapter 0338, at a gate, and she says one thing over her shoulder about not having written a name at the top of anything.**

**AND NOBODY IN THESE TEN DAYS GOT STRONGER.** Adrian is Stage 2 on all ten. The gains are a man who read his own sheet and said nothing for four days, a yes nobody asked for, nine words nobody has said, a skylight and a window, eight of nineteen stones and nobody able to say when, a doorway nobody turned a person out at, a refusal of the only page that would have worked, a winter, four doors and a name for a piece of ground, a notice on the rough side of a board because there was no form, a man who will not put his hands on it, a second bar at a gate, a date said out loud to a boy, a rule about words on a wall, and a name off a board that nobody had been told about. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.**

---

## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005 — the state of every person this batch put on a page, days 341 to 350

**The authoritative block for the current phase is the one headed *VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005*, above, and the next phase is `workspace/volume-07/close/PROMPT.md`. Everything above this block in this file is history and is superseded by it.**

**ADRIAN VALE.** **Named in ONE of the ten — Chapter 0346, a room over a market in which he reads an entry in a register and the empty third column and does not touch either, and is asked no question about it — and in NO CHAPTER IN ANY FORM in the other nine.** The rule is that he is named in the chapters in which he is reading a page in a room, and a chapter that has a page in it and nobody in the room identified does not count, and this one has a man identified. He is aged nowhere in Volume 07. He is Stage 2 on all fifty days. He performed no working, no threshold was opened, the Narrow Mark is not opened a third time, the other world is not named once across the fifty files, nobody offered him a workway and he asked for none, and the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero. **He is in Chapter 0341, 0344 and 0349 in a room or a field where people do not ask him anything, and that is a different count from the number of chapters he is named in, and the two must not be merged.** **The consent fracture is visible in Chapter 0349 and is not mended and nobody apologised in it: she says he has not asked for the sheet since the second day and that she has noticed every day for nineteen days, and he says he is not going to ask her and is not going to put it in a room, and the fifth condition is not given.** The thing he brought and did not use this batch: a hand on a slate, a finger's width from an empty column, and nothing else.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL AND WROTE THE NINTH LINE.** Chapters 0342 and 0345 and in no others of these ten. **Second handle: a piece of chalk he does not use, and it is the same piece in both chapters and it is the same piece he carried in the inside breast pocket from the fifty-fifth day of the Short Month.** **HE IS ON NO ROSTER IN THIS CITY AND HAS ASKED TO BE ON NONE OF THEM, and a man with a piece of chalk he does not use and no standing is the climax of this volume, and the two are the same person and Chapter 0342 is able to say both.** He is barred from every roster at his own request, he had no standing to take anything back, and the instrument that removed a word from a page in this world is a room, in daylight, with a date on it, and a person. **He said the word in Chapter 0310, in one word, in the local language, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door, and did not say what it means, and he took it off the second slate in Chapter 0342 in chalk with a cloth, and he did not use the chalk, because a slate is the one surface in four hundred miles a word can be taken off and a wall nine miles up the road is not.** His reason is four sentences and it is his. He was at the stair in Chapter 0342 and would not be thanked and would not be talked to on a stair. **The ninth line was not moved, dated, read aloud, taken down, thanked for or resolved; he refused it a sixth time in Chapter 0345, he asked in advance not to be thanked and was not thanked, and whether it works is unanswered.** He is not a villain and is not the hero of Chapter 0342 and is not converted and is not leaving.

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER.** Chapters 0342 and 0346 and in no others of these ten, and the two women of twenty-nine were never in one room and nothing in this batch settles whether they are one woman. **Second handle: her second slate, and the register, and a piece of chalk she put down beside the slate and did not pick up again.** She has three columns in her form and the third one is where a person goes, and nine years of entries have somebody's name in it. **In Chapter 0342 she put the slate on the table herself, said out loud that she can do this in a room in daylight with a date on the door and that is the whole of what she can do, that she cannot enter it because there is nothing to enter and she could not enter it if there were a column, and that the third thing cannot be done at all and everybody in the room has been working for a fortnight on the assumption that it can.** **In Chapter 0346 she ruled a line and wrote the thirty-first day of that month at the top and, underneath it, that a straight mark in a field may not be dug, with no name and no word for the mark and the third column left empty on purpose, and said out loud that she will leave the room rather than fill it, and that she is not going to write down what the word was even though she wrote it down once.** She told the man of about fifty-seven what he has, in the order of the facts. She did not ask anybody anything and nobody asked her anything.

**TAMSN QUILL.** **Chapter 0349 and in no other of these ten, and this is the only chapter of the last ten days in which she is on a page.** Second handle: **a folded sheet of road with about nine places on it, folded the same way every time, and a ninth line with nothing on it.** She did not put the ninth place in the nine and nobody saw her leave it out and everybody in the room understood it inside about four minutes. **She has had the sheet in the inside of her coat since the fifteenth of the Bare Month, which is nineteen days, and she has been in this room and that room and has not been asked for it once, and the consent fracture is visible and is not mended and nobody apologised in it.** She said she knows he is not going to ask, and that he has not asked since the second day, and that she has noticed every day, and that she is not asking him why, and that she would like it said in the room that she said she was not asking. He said he is not going to put it in a room because a room has a date on the door and everything that goes in it is on a page by the fourth hour. **She is the lead's want — the Lock of Salt and the Hearth Marches from being claimed — and this volume was hers and he followed it, and her want is not asked for and not answered in these ten days.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY AT THE SHUT GATE.** Chapters 0341 and 0347 and in no others of these ten. **Second handle, the same in both: a brass key on a tarred cord in the apron pocket, a bar of ash in two brackets, a wooden hook, a piece of chalk, and a date in chalk on the post every morning with nothing rubbed out.** **She gives no name and no name may be given her. NOBODY IN EITHER CHAPTER ASKS HER ANYTHING, INCLUDING BY THE PROTAGONIST, AND NOBODY WAS SENT TO HER FOR ANY REASON, and the carrier went up that lane on the Saturday with nothing in his hands so that if anybody did ask him he would be able to say nothing at all.** She put the twenty-sixth on the post and then the thirty-second, and neither of them rubbed anything out, and about four people on that lane have worked out since that a column of dates with nothing under it is the same shape as the entry that went into a book in this city on Thursday. **The one thing only she and the man with the barrow know is not said in Chapter 0341 and is not said in Chapter 0347 and is not said anywhere in fifty chapters, and the two of them have never spoken about it and neither of them is going to be asked.** She said out loud to a man's back that she is not going to be asked when the boards come off that wall, and she said out loud to the man with the barrow that she is not going to be asked about the boards and is not going to offer anybody anything about the boards, and neither of those is a question. **The cost of the not-asking is not hers and Chapter 0341 says so.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT WITH THE BARROW.** Chapters 0341 and 0347 and in no others of these ten. **Second handle: a barrow, with the rope hooked back over the handle where he puts it.** He came up the lane on the twenty-sixth of the Bare Month, went past the gate, put the barrow down about nine hundred paces off it, stood with his forearms on the handles, looked at one side of the road, did not go in, and came back down and stopped at the boards on the thirty-second, which is the first time in about a month he has stopped at that gate. **He is the only person in four hundred miles who knows that the woman at that gate saw a straight line in that orchard and told him and nobody else, and the chapter does not explain it and does not hint at it in a way that explains it, and he was not asked about it in either chapter and is not going to be.** He said the whole of what he did on the Saturday because nobody was going to ask him for it, and he did not say what he stood there for. He took nine boards off a wall one at a time in the wrong order and lost a quarter of an hour, and the two soft ones went on the bottom of the barrow.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO WHO DIGS.** **Chapter 0344 and in no other of these ten, and it is the chapter he goes in.** Second handle: **a rod he does not carry — a length of ash about four feet long with a flat blade on the end of it, ground down on one side and not the other — standing against the thick wall of his own hut eleven miles the other way, and he offered the fact of it out loud in a room on the twenty-fourth before anybody asked him for it and did not bring it.** He walked eleven miles. **He put a barrow against the end of a cut at a stone about nine hundred paces off a shut gate, and looked along a mark in the ground the whole way from the end of it, and did not touch it, and did not put a foot in it and did not stand over it and did not go along it, and came back four paces at a time.** He said one sentence and it was to the man with the handcart and not to the field: he cannot tell you one thing about it that he did not know at the seventh hour, and he would like that said out loud before anybody asks him what he saw, and that is all he has and he has walked eleven miles. **The sentence is not an answer. Nothing was measured, nothing was dug, nothing was counted, no rod went into anything, and he is the only character in the last ten days who goes and looks at the line.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY WITH A HANDCART AND A LENGTH OF ROPE.** Chapters 0344, 0348 and 0350, and in no others of these ten. **Second handle: a handcart and a length of rope that has been used and is not new.** He was told no about a fence across the top of that road in the first week of that month, said he would come back, has come back four times, and **he was in Chapter 0344 at the end of the same cut the digger stood at and he built nothing that morning and nobody sent him.** He cut sixteen willow posts in a copse about four miles the other way and stacked them in a cart, set out from a row of trees, stopped at a stone, and did not measure anything: **four feet is a number he has in his mouth and not a number he took off anything, and he said so out loud, and a fence is not an account of anything and does not know what it is four feet off.** He put nine posts in the ground on the Saturday with seven left in the cart, and thirteen by the Monday with three left, and said he would finish on the Thursday. **Nobody sent him and nobody stops him and it will be there on Tuesday.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN.** Chapters 0341, 0343, 0345, 0346, 0348, 0349, 0350. **Second handle: a length of new rope he has not used, and a folded paper with a sum on it and his name at the top of it, which is the only sheet in four hundred miles with his name on it and which he has been carrying since before the water.** He bought half a holding about forty years ago. He asked four doors in a row where their own ground stops and not one of them knows. **He said the naming bought that field one afternoon and cost that field thirty-two days and that he is not going to be told it is a sad one. He was told, in a room in daylight with a date on the door, what he has: a line he cannot have shown anybody and a winter he cannot get back, and the only page that would have shown anybody what he paid for is one he refused on the nineteenth and was right to refuse. He said the cost out loud and nobody in that room told him he was wrong. He is not a villain in this volume, is not converted, does not leave, is not happy, and is right about most of what he says. He could have stopped a fence and did not move out of the way and then moved, and he put his hand flat on the second post the way a man does to a gate post and did not press on it.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE.** **Chapter 0346 and in no other of these ten. Second handle: a tin, carried a mile in both hands, and it was empty.** She came up from the end of the lane at about the eighth hour and nobody had sent for her, which is the only two kinds of coming there is. **Her sheet of paper thirty years old, in her own hand, with nothing at the top of it and no day on it and no name on it, has been on a table in a room over a market since the fifth of that month and has not been moved and has not been copied and does not go out of the room, and the condition she put on it on the fifth is hers and nobody may un-condition it.** She did not look at it a second time. **She asked, once, in that room, on that day, and nobody answered it: what is a person for, on a page, when the only thing anybody needs them for is a journey.** That is the question this volume hands on and it is on a page and it is not answered.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX WHO HOLDS GATES ON A TURN.** **Chapter 0345 and in no other of these ten. Second handle, the same in every chapter he is in: a bar stood against a post, a barrow with a spade lying across its handles, and a bootful emptied before he answers.** He put a second bar in a bracket of his own making on a Wednesday night because a gate that does not shut is not a gate, and nobody sent for it. **A roll in the Marches is a gate, a keeper, a set of days and nothing else, and a gate with two bars is one gate and goes on the roll as one gate, and the second one is on no roll at all, and no clerk four hundred miles off is going to come up that road and count them.** He said he was not going to say what that is worth and that it is not because he does not know. **A keeper is not a holder, the word is at zero across the fifty files, and the two rolls of the same gates are not put side by side.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-FIVE.** Chapters 0342 and 0349 and in no others of these ten. **Second handle: a left boot mended at the front with a length of cord that is not new, and nine words in the inside of his coat that he wrote himself on the eleventh of the Bare Month and has not said in any room.** **He was standing in the room on the twenty-seventh of the Bare Month with a person beside him when a word came off a slate in a cloth, and he did not say them, and nobody asked him, and he did not say them in Chapter 0349 either.** He and the woman of about twenty-one had the ninth line of a sheet of road between them on a table and neither of them put it there in front of anybody. **The nine words are on a page at the end of this volume and they are a live thread.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-ONE.** Chapters 0342 and 0349 and in no others of these ten. **Second handle: a pen with the nib ground narrow, cut down with a knife, and she has not written anything with it in any room she has been in since the fifteenth of the Bare Month.** **She said on the fifteenth that she had stopped waiting to be told what she is for, and on the thirty-fourth she said it again in a shorter form, and the second time is not a complaint, and the not-waiting is not a mood and nobody in the room is going to change it by saying something.** She is standing in Chapter 0342 while a word comes off a slate and she is not asked anything.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT.** Chapters 0342 and 0349 and in no others of these ten. **Second handle: a folding knife with a hinge that has gone stiff and does not spring, which he opens and shuts twice with his thumb and that will then hold about nine minutes.** He said yes before anybody in that room had finished asking, in Chapter 0332, and he was not asked anything about it, and he did not hand the knife to anybody in Chapter 0342 when he was asked to hold a pen.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO CARRIES THINGS FOR A LIVING.** Chapters 0341, 0342, 0343, 0344, 0347, 0348, 0350. He has carried five sheets in this flood and has not opened one. **He said the one sentence that made the Monday afternoon worse, and it was to the room and not to the man, and it was: a man told this room yesterday that there is a man with a barrow who has been up that lane; a man who digs is going to stand at the end of a cut in that field on a day not one of us knows; the word came off a slate in this room on Sunday because of the first of those; there is no form in four hundred miles that holds a thing and what it costs.** He has said out loud that he is not going to ask whether it will go, and he has said it in about nine rooms in a fortnight, and a woman who keeps a register told him it is not a sentence about whether anything works and never was, and he said it has never once done the slightest good and he is going to keep saying it. **He came up that lane on the Saturday with nothing in his hands so that if anybody did ask him he would be able to say nothing at all.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-THREE WHO KEEPS A ROOM.** Chapters 0341, 0345, 0348. She has a bread board at the end of a sill. **In Chapter 0341 she said the one thing she was allowed to say in this city, and it was to the man of about forty-three and not to the room: he has had the deed out and the sheet off it in front of about nine people three times in about a fortnight, and each time he has been right about wanting it and wrong about why, and she has not said so and is not going to be asked.** **She is the same woman as the one in Chapters 0331, 0332, 0333 and 0339, and she is the one who carried the order of nineteen stones in on the seventeenth of that month and has been out and looked at them more than once, and she stands at the third post of a fence in Chapter 0348 with her basket on the wall and says nothing, and the paragraph that keeps the nineteen stones and the fence apart names her as the one who has been out and looked at them. THE IDENTIFICATION IS ON A PAGE IN CHAPTER 0332 AND IS NOT A MERGE: she is the only person in four hundred miles who is connected to both, and neither of the two things is joined to the other, and a later chapter may not join them, may not resolve the stones and may not make her say anything about the fence.**

**AND NOBODY IN THESE TEN DAYS GOT STRONGER.** Adrian is Stage 2 on all ten. The gains are a word off a slate, thirty-two days, a barrow at the end of a cut, a sentence that is not an answer, a second bar on no roll, a refusal of what it is worth twice, an entry with an empty third column, nine boards and a bare wall, thirteen posts and a fence, and a hand on a gate post. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.**

**AND THE PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS, AND ARE NOT TO BE PUT IN THEM BY A LATER WRITER.** The man of about thirty-four at the wharf with the tally of the four hundred and forty. The man who trades on the Open Hand board, who is given the trade and the board and NO AGE. Hettis Roke, forty-four, nineteen years in. The man of about forty-one in a very good coat, and the sheet he will not turn over, which is not the Crown's schedule. The keeper of the Redroot gate, whose days are not counted and whose year is not named. The girl of seventeen. The man of about twenty-seven and the board. The woman of about thirty-four who keeps a stall by a wharf, who is a different person from the man of about thirty-four who keeps a stall, and **no paragraph of Volume 07 carries the two of them together.**

## THE VOLUME 07 CLOSE, AND ITS REVIEW REPAIR PASS — the authoritative block for the current phase, and it wins over everything above it

**The next phase is `workspace/volume-08/outline/PROMPT.md`, which is a VOLUME OUTLINE PHASE and not a batch.** The block this header named until now, *VOLUME 07, BATCH 0004 — ten days, ten people, and nobody in them got stronger*, was two phases stale and is history, as is *VOLUME 07, BATCH 0005*. `state/volume-07-close.md` is the volume's inheritance and it carries the full record at its §5 and §8. **Where this file and a chapter disagree about a day, a name or a figure, the chapter is right.** It is over 400 KB because forty-one batches and two close phases have appended to it; read this block and not the file.

**THE REPAIR PASS CHANGED ONE PERSON'S AGE AND ONE PERSON'S CHAPTER, AND NOTHING ELSE ABOUT ANYBODY, and that is the whole of what a person-shaped correction in this repository should look like.**

**THE MAN WHO TRADES ON THE OPEN HAND BOARD. THIRTY-NINE, settled, and the page now says so.** He was given the trade, the board, the nineteen rates, the four markets and the four years on `chapters/volume-03/chapter-0123.md`, and the age came with them, and the Volume 07 outline gave him *thirty-seven* by merging him with another man. **The age is thirty-nine: `man of about thirty-nine` stands 133 times in 69 files word-bounded, against 6 in 1 for `man of about thirty-seven`.** **Second handle: a board — he has a board of his own under his arm, and nineteen rates on it, and he never once carries it with the writing on the side the rain can get at.** **What is on a page: he refused the volume's one panel in about four seconds in Chapter 0340, in a room with a date on the door, and the cost was specific and he paid it — he had put a person on that board and taken them off it in his own hand in about the ninth year of this flood and had never said so to anybody, and saying so out loud in a room in daylight is what refusing cost him.** He is not defeated. He has said in a mouth that a word can go on a thing in a room and come off it again in a room, and that a person cannot come off at all. **What was withdrawn from him in the repair pass: the descriptor *is a return*, which belongs to the man of about fifty-seven, and the attribution of his refusal to Chapter 0334, which is not his chapter.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN IS NOT AVAILABLE TO ANYBODY, and this is a prohibition and not a gap.** He is a real person on a page: `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0293.md` at lines 15, 33, 61, 75 and 83, and he comes in behind a woman of about thirty-seven, carries a sack, does not open it, and stands in eight kitchens in a week working out whether four is a number anybody should be given tonight. **He is a question-carrier and not a board-trader. He may not be given a trade, a board, a market or a refusal, and no later writer may put him and the board-trader in a room, because nothing on a page has ever connected them and the descriptor that did was a counting error.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN, AND THE THINGS THE REPAIR PASS GAVE BACK TO HIM.** He paid money for half a holding about forty years ago through a house four hundred miles inland and cannot show anybody where it is. **Second handle: a length of new rope he has not used, invented on the page in Chapter 0307.** **He has answered a return four times a year since before this flood — the descriptor the repair pass took off the board-trader — and he is the man who refused a page with his own name at the top of it in Chapter 0334**, *A Sheet With A Name At The Top Of It, Refused*, at `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0334.md:19` and `:27`, **and what it cost is a season and a certainty, and he is right, and nobody in that room told him he was wrong.** He is not defeated and is not going to be. *Open.*

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-ONE. THE NO IS NOT A FIGURE AND IS NOT TO BE SOFTENED, EXPLAINED, ARGUED WITH, APOLOGISED FOR, OVERTURNED OR MADE A DEFEAT.** Day 329, a Monday, the fourteenth of the Bare Month, a room with a date on the door, about nine people: *I am not going to.* The first thing she said in that room was that nobody had asked her anything at all, and that she was sixty-one, and that she would like that said out loud in a room with a date on the door, and then she was going to say the other thing, and the other thing was hers and she was not going to be asked for it either. **On the eighteenth, Chapter 0333, she came to the door of the room she was written into and did not come in, and three people in that room did not get up and did not turn round, and the door stood open for as long as it takes to fill a pail after she had gone.** She carried the fourth notice to that door herself. **Nobody is going to ask her about it and nobody after this volume may ask her about it either unless she asks first, and she is not going to.**

**THE FOUR PEOPLE WHO HAVE NOT LEFT IN TWO HUNDRED AND FIVE DAYS, at day 350, which is derived and on no page and in no mouth.** Three said yes in their own words. The fourth is not a hole in a party of four.

**AND NOBODY IN THIS VOLUME GOT STRONGER, and the shape of that is a standing fact and not a note about these ten days.** Adrian Vale is Stage 2 on all fifty days, performed no working, no threshold was opened, the Narrow Mark is not opened a third time, the other world is not named once across fifty files, nobody offered him a workway and he asked for none, and *passage* and *privilege* are at zero. **He is named in ONE of the volume's last ten chapters, Chapter 0346, and in no chapter in any form in the other nine, and the one is a room in which he is reading a page.** The Volume 08 midpoint puts him in a yard working something out, **and the chapter says he worked it out and does not say he was right, and that is the whole of what is permitted.** The gains are a month with a name and no length, a name read off an index in four seconds and not put on anything, seven notices on seven days with nothing at the top of them, a yes nobody asked for, nine words nobody has said, eight of nineteen stones and nobody able to say when, a skylight, a door nobody turned a person out at, a refusal of the only page that would have worked, four doors in a row and not one person who can say where their own ground stops, a second bar on no roll, a rule about words, a name off a board, a word put to a line in the ground and taken back off the only page it was ever on, thirty-two days, a sentence that is not an answer, a line that may not be dug, nine boards off a wall, and a fence. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.**

**AND THE FOUR NEW RULES THAT BIND ANY VOLUME AFTER THIS ONE, which are about instruments and not about people and are recorded here because this file is where a writer looks for what a character may not do.** **The mean is a consequence and not a plan, and no volume may read its own bolded share as a quality figure and no review may fail a volume on one.** **A paragraph carrying neither speaker marker is invisible to both checks that measure speech, and the count of them must be zero.** **A set of objects counted in two chapters is counted by no check at all, and the fence of sixteen posts stood at three totals across Chapters 0348 and 0350 until a repair closed it, so a later writer may not print a count that a neighbouring chapter prints differently.** **And a descriptor map counts occurrences and does not decide which occurrence is the same person, and the decision about that is an outline's or a close phase's and not a batch's — the two merges this repository has actually committed were committed by outline and close decisions, not by a chapter.**

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/outline/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A VOLUME OUTLINE PHASE AND NOT A BATCH.** It writes `outline/volume-08.md` and the first Volume 08 batch's cards and no prose, it inherits four decisions of record in `outline/series.md` which are the gate, and it creates exactly one next phase, which is the batch that writes Chapter 0351.

## THE VOLUME 08 OUTLINE — the authoritative block for the current phase, and it wins over everything above it

**The next phase is `workspace/volume-08/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, which is a BATCH and not an outline.** The block this header named until now is history. `state/volume-07-close.md` is Volume 07's inheritance. `outline/volume-08.md` §6.4, §6.6, §6.7 and §20.8 are the cast decisions of record. **Where this file and a chapter disagree about a day, a name, a descriptor or a figure, the chapter is right.** This block records what a writer looks here for, which is what a person may not do.

**THE TWO PEOPLE VOLUME 08 ADDS WHO ARE ON NO PAGE, each measured at ZERO across Volume 07's fifty files before it was taken, with the 350-file figure beside it because a low 350-file figure is the best guard against a merge that a word-bounded count cannot see.**

**A MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-NINE. One sentence in one file across three hundred and fifty. He walks the four-hundred-mile road inland with a barrow and has walked it four times in nine years. In the ninth year of this flood he came up that road and found the bar on a gate at the head of it rotted through, and he put in a bar and hung a hurdle on it, and nobody sent him and nobody has a form for what he did. He is on no page until Chapter 0361. Second handle: a hurdle he made and a bar he replaced, invented on the page in Chapter 0361, and the same in every chapter he is in after it. He is the last image of the volume's man and he is not going to be asked about the gate, and no chapter of Volume 08 may ask him what the four feet is off.**

**A WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO. Seven sentences in six files across three hundred and fifty and zero in Volume 07. She keeps a household of about nine on the low ground. She is asked for her days on day 356, in a kitchen, in daylight, with a date on the door, by the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register, and she refuses the fourth place out loud in about four seconds, and the cost is named first and it is hers: that she has said no to a thing in a room twice in nine years and the second one cost her a season's worth of being told nothing. She is not the woman of Chapter 0266 and the two are never in one room. Second handle: a door she is not asked to open, invented on the page in Chapter 0356. She does not define the word *share* and says in a mouth that she is not going to.**

**AND THE DESCRIPTOR THIS VOLUME DELIBERATELY AVOIDS, which is the most useful thing in this block. *Man of about forty-four* is at zero in Volume 07 and stands at 162 sentences in 85 files across the three hundred and fifty, and a descriptor that is at zero in one volume and enormous in the six before it is a descriptor with a person attached to it that no page has ever named — which is the exact shape of the fault the two new rules about instruments were written for, and the shape of the merge the Volume 07 outline actually made.** A man of about thirty-nine is used and is not avoided, because he is on a page 133 times in 69 files and the age is settled and there is nothing to avoid. The four crowded descriptors in the 350-file map are *woman of about thirty-four* at 351 in 142, *man of about thirty-four* at 283 in 108, *man of about forty-four* at 162 in 85 and *man of about thirty-nine* at 133 in 69. **And the standing co-presence prohibition holds: no paragraph of Volume 08 may carry the man of about thirty-four and a woman of about thirty-four together. On a word-bounded search it is at zero and on an unbounded one it returns twenty, and every one of the twenty is a false positive because *woman of about thirty-four* contains the string.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT, AND THE TWO OF HIM, AND THE DECISION THIS PHASE MADE.** **They are two people and a chapter may not merge them.** One is on the boards of a salt wharf in this city with a tally-board, a knife, a barrow and about four hundred and forty on it, defined at `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0308.md:3`; he has put a number in a return four times a year since the winter before the water and has never been asked what the column is for, and **on day 357, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, he is asked it by the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register, and he answers, in the local language, out loud, once, in four to eight words, and what he says is the name of this volume. That phrase is on a page in Chapter 0357 and in no file, and no batch record, no prompt and no outline may record it.** The figure he has, as against the figure he sends, is on no page and is printed nowhere in this volume. **On day 393 he is the one who refuses the volume's one panel in about four seconds, and the cost is that the panel has told that room that nobody had to be told, and he says so in six words, and nobody improves on the six words and nobody thanks him.** A chapter may not put the two acts in the same room on the same day and may not have him say he knows what he said.

The other came up a lane with a barrow of boards and a rope hooked back over the handle, and took nine boards off a wall on day 347, and is at `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0312.md`, `:0341.md` and `:0347.md`. **He is not in Chapter 0357 and may not be put in the room where it happens, and the empty barrow he comes up the lane with is a declared motif and is not evidence that the two men are one.** The decision is at `outline/volume-08.md` §20.9.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN, AND WHAT THIS VOLUME MAY NOT DO TO HIM.** He paid money for half a holding about forty years ago and cannot show anybody where it is, and he is right about most of what he says, and he is not defeated and is not going to be. He has answered a return four times a year since before this flood. He refused the only page in four hundred miles that would have got his winter back in Chapter 0334. **He is standing in front of a fence that is now finished, and the volume's new question is about that fence, and it is asked in a room on day 396 and it is NOT asked of him, and a chapter that has him answer it has taken the volume's only new question and put it in the mouth of a man who has already refused the only page that would have worked.** He does not leave, he is not converted and he is not happy. Second handle: a length of new rope he has not used, on a page since Chapter 0307, and **that rope is his and the man of about sixty's rope is a used one and is not new, and a card may not join them.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY, WHO FINISHES A FENCE.** Sixteen posts cut, nine in the ground on day 348, four more on day 350, the last three on **day 353, a Thursday**, and eleven withies. He began at a row of trees because a row of trees is a thing you can see from a road, and he did not measure anything, and about four feet is a number he has in his mouth. **Nobody sent him, nobody has a form for it, nobody asks him, and no chapter of Volume 08 may ask him anything.** Second handle: a hazel mallet with its head bound in cord, and a coil of rope, invented on the page in Chapter 0353.

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-ONE WITH THE PEN WITH THE NIB GROUND NARROW, CUT DOWN WITH A KNIFE.** She walked nine places in three days and wrote nothing on the ninth line and has stopped waiting to be told what she is for, and she is one of the four, and she said yes in her own words on day 330. **On day 375, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, Tamsin Quill asks her whether it will go, for the first time in this flood, and the asking is the event and nothing else in the chapter is.** **On day 393 a page with her name at the top of it goes out on a date, and the man of about thirty-nine is right that the day after that there is a column, and the page goes anyway, and she is two days down a road and is not rescued and is not defeated and asked for nothing.** **She may not be made to start waiting to be told what she is for again, and a chapter may not have her say she does not know what she is for if that is the same sentence in a different mouth.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-ONE. THE NO IS NOT A FIGURE AND IS NOT TO BE SOFTENED, EXPLAINED, ARGUED WITH, APOLOGISED FOR, OVERTURNED OR MADE A DEFEAT.** Carried forward whole from `state/volume-07-close.md` §8.2, which is the version to read. **AND NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 08 MAY COUNT THE DAYS SINCE SHE SAID NO, AT ANY VALUE, IN NARRATION OR IN A MOUTH.** She is not a hole in a party of four and she is not asked.

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE, WHO ASKS THE SECOND QUESTION.** She holds a folded sheet thirty years old in a tin, the condition she attached to it on the fifth of that month is hers, and it is not entered, not copied and does not leave the building. **She asked *what is a person for, on a page, when the only thing anybody needs them for is a journey* on day 346 and got no answer. On day 396 she asks *who is a fence for, when the two people it stands between never agreed where it was*, to the room and not to anybody, and gets no answer, and the rhyme is a decision and not an accident.**

**AND THE PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT IN VOLUME 08, AND ARE NOT TO BE PUT IN IT BY A LATER WRITER.** The keeper of the Redroot gate, whose days are not counted and whose year is not named. The girl of seventeen, who is not fetched and whose being unfindable is not a qualification. The man of about thirty-seven in `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0293.md`, who is a real person on a page and is not the board-trader and may not be given a trade, a board, a market or a refusal. Hettis Roke, forty-four, nineteen years in. A woman of forty-eight in a fourth house at Nine Elms. Jonah Pike. The man of about thirty-two on the bank. The Regent. Ivenn Marrow, whose placement in Volume 08 is a debt and not a page.

**AND NOBODY IN THIS VOLUME GETS STRONGER, and the shape of that is a standing fact.** Adrian Vale is Stage 2 on all fifty days, performs no working, opens no threshold, does not name the other world once, is offered no workway and asks for none, is aged nowhere, and *passage* and *privilege* are at zero. **He is named in the chapters where he reads a page and in no chapter where people decline to ask him: **Chapters 0352, 0355 and 0360 in the first batch, and 0371, 0376, 0381 and 0392 in the later ones — seven of fifty, and NOT 0358 and NOT 0359, which were in an earlier draft of this block and should not have been**, and Chapter 0376 is a yard in which he works something out and the chapter says he worked it out and does not say he was right.** The gains of this volume are a return read out, a fourth place identified, a man asked what a column is for and answering, a fence finished at sixteen posts, a man asked for the first time in this flood whether it will go, three people on a road, a name at the top of a page, a panel refused, a line with a day on it and nothing under it, and a fence at the end of a road. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.**

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A BATCH. It writes Chapters 0351 to 0360, days 351 to 360, and it is told that a batch does not decide what a volume is, does not invent a person, does not answer any of the questions above, and does not record in any file it produces the words the man of about thirty-eight speaks in Chapter 0357.**

# VOLUME 08, BATCH 0001 — the authoritative block for the current phase

**This block wins over everything above it. Its record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0001.md` and its ten chapters are on a page. Where this file and a chapter disagree about a person, a descriptor, a day or a figure, the chapter is right. This block is what a writer looks here for, which is what a person may not do, and it records what the first ten days of Volume 08 did to the people in it and what it did not do.**

**A WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO IS ON A PAGE, AND SHE IS NOT THE WOMAN OF CHAPTER 0266, AND THE TWO HAVE NEVER BEEN IN ONE ROOM.** She keeps a household of about nine on the low ground, behind a wall of about nine houses with one date in chalk on it and nothing above it, and on the forty-first day of that month, a Sunday, a sheet that had gone to the wall on the Friday morning for a room on the Tuesday was two days old and on the third day of the four, and she came out to a door, which nobody on that wall has done. **She refused the fourth place out loud in about four seconds and the cost was named first and it is hers: that nobody has ever asked her for her days, and that a number has not been said out loud to anybody in nine years, and that she is not going to be thanked for a no.** She did not define the word and said out loud that she was not going to. She did not open the door for anybody and nobody asked her to; the register-keeper went in because she was invited in and not because she had knocked. **Her second handle, invented on the page in Chapter 0356, is a door she is not asked to open.** Her finding is her own and it is not the finding of the woman of Chapter 0266 and is not that finding said differently: a share is a thing you have got and a place in it is a thing you have not got, and a person asked to put the first into the second has been asked to give up the only one of the two that is hers. **A child came into that kitchen twice and was not spoken to either time and about nine people heard every word of it and not one of them was asked anything.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT SPOKE, ONCE, ON DAY 357, AND WHAT HE SAID IS THE NAME OF THIS VOLUME AND IS IN NO FILE.** He was asked in the room over a market, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door, about nine people in it, by the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register, and the reason she gave was nine words and she did not say it twice, and he had put a number in a return four times a year since the winter before the water and had never been asked. **He answered in the local language, out loud, once, in seven words, and neither of them stopped working while it happened, and nobody thanked him, and he did not ask to be thanked, and he was not asked again and did not want to be.** His second handle is a tally-board, a knife, a barrow and about nine pounds of salt off the boards in a basket, all of it on a page already. **The figure he has in his head, as against the figure he sends, is on no page, is printed in none of the ten chapters and in no file this batch produced, and is not available to anybody.** **On day 393 he is the one who refuses the volume's one panel, and the cost is that the panel tells that room nobody had to be told, and nobody thanks him for either act and no chapter puts the two in one room on one day.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR DID NOT TAKE THE CHALK OUT, ONCE, IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE, AND WAS NOT THANKED.** He is on no roster in this city at his own asking. He is on a page in Chapter 0354 with a piece of chalk in an inside breast pocket he has carried since the forty-third day of the Short Month, and the room wanted a heading written on a board over its door and about nine people were going to write one and he was not going to be the one who did it. **He asked in advance not to be thanked and nobody thanked him. He said one true thing about the form and it was not a rule and it was not an answer, and the chapter says so in the same line.** The ninth line was not moved, not dated, not read aloud, not taken down, not thanked for and not resolved, and whether it works stays unanswered.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE WAS NOT ASKED ANYTHING, AND HE SAID TWO SPEECHES IN THE LAST HOUR OF A ROOM, AND HIS SLATE WAS NEVER OPENED.** In Chapter 0355 he is against the wall by the step at the back where the light does not reach, with his own slate on his knee and his right hand flat over the face of it, and he does not speak in the first hour, and about four people in that room worked out that he had been waiting all morning to be asked what a board is for and that he had arranged never to be asked. **He says two things and no more: *that is the whole of it*, and then a form, a heading, four places, and the fourth of them belonging to somebody who is not in this room, and that every one of the four is correct, and that this is the first room in about a fortnight where he was told what the last of the four was out loud before anybody asked him for it, and that he is not going to be asked about it. He was not thanked and nobody improved on him.** **THE COUNT IS TWO AND THE FIGURE THIS BLOCK PUBLISHED WAS FOUR, and Chapter 0355's own two closing paragraphs said *four things* and *four sentences* while the chapter prints two speeches, and a previous edition of this block also carried a sentence about *two words of an answer to the fourth place and the other two kept*, which is in no file and is not on the page; it is withdrawn and what it described is the two speeches above.** The chapter that settles what he is worth is Chapter 0393 and is not this batch's.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY FINISHED A FENCE AND WAS NOT ASKED ONE QUESTION AND WAS NOT THANKED AND IS NOT ON A PAGE BY NAME.** Sixteen posts, nine on the thirty-third day of that month, four more on the thirty-fifth, the last three on the thirty-eighth, eleven withies. **He said two sentences on the third morning, one of them a number he had left and one of them a thing he had noticed about himself, and neither of them was about a boundary, and the second of them is the reason there was a third morning at all.** He does not say the four feet out loud again and it is in narration once, in Chapter 0353, as a number a man has in his mouth, and not in a mouth at all. His second handle is a hazel mallet with its head bound in cord and a coil of rope that has been used and is not new, **and that rope is his and the man of about fifty-seven's rope is new and has never been used and the two ropes have never been in the same cart and are not going to be.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN WAS NOT DEFEATED, WAS NOT CONVERTED, DID NOT LEAVE AND IS NOT HAPPY, AND THE VOLUME'S NEW QUESTION WAS NOT ASKED OF HIM.** In Chapter 0360, in a room with a date on the door, in front of about nine people, he said out loud that a page with his own name at the top of it is a page somebody can ask for and that the day after that there is a column and that he has been a column twice in his life and both times it was somebody else's. Nobody told him he was wrong, because nobody in that room is wrong. He also said, of the only instrument this world has for saying who is going on a journey, that there is no form in four hundred miles for it. **He refused nothing in that chapter. Nobody asked him where the fence is, he was not told, and nobody in the ten chapters asked him anything about the line. THE NUMBER OF DAYS THE FOUR OF THEM HAVE NOT LEFT IS ON A PAGE IN THAT CHAPTER IN NARRATION AND IN NO MOUTH, and the chapter's next line is that nobody in that room said the figure out loud. A previous edition of this block said he refused the figure out loud, told her not to say it in that room and not to put it on the board, and gave the reason that it was still a figure he had had for about forty days; no such speech is in Chapter 0360 or in any of the ten, and it is withdrawn.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE CARRIED NINE THINGS IN THIS FLOOD AND OPENED NONE OF THEM, AND HE WAS TOLD HE WOULD BE ASKED SOMETHING AND WAS NOT.** Five sheets in Volume 07, a form that came up the road on the thirty-sixth day of that month, and three notices — for the fortieth, the forty-third and the forty-eighth days of that month. **He gave a reason once, on eleven miles of open ground in the Short Month, that a question in your own hand is worth more than a question in yours, and that the reason is nine years old and is his, and he has not said it since.** He stood in a shut road four times in a fortnight watching work get finished by people who were not sent, **and on the way back from the last of them he did not stop at a shut gate, did not knock, did not put a hand on the boards and did not ask the woman of about fifty who keeps it one question, and she said one thing to a post and it was a date.** His second handle is a satchel with a flap.

**THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WASHES A BOARD EVERY MORNING AND HAS DECIDED NOT TO WASH THE WALL ABOVE IT, AND HIS REASON IS ABOUT HOW A THING LOOKS AND NOT ABOUT WHAT IS ON IT.** The board is nine miles up the upper road at the top of a store, it has not been written on since the two hundred and twenty-fifth day of this flood and the figures on it are the figures of the day before that, **and this batch moved it out of a room over a market and put it back where Chapter 0336 has it, and no figure about it moved.** In Chapter 0354 he came down the road with a pail and a cloth and washed a second board, the room's own, which carries the smudge of about nine dates, and nobody sent for him and he did not say why he came.

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-ONE WITH THE PEN WITH THE NIB GROUND NARROW, CUT DOWN WITH A KNIFE, IS IN ONE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND WAS NOT ASKED ANYTHING.** She is in Chapter 0358, in a room over a market on the forty-third day of that month, and she came up the stair without him, and about four people in that room worked out that the two of them have been in a documented check since the seventeenth day of the Short Month and that she is in a room on a Tuesday and he is not in it, and that nobody in that room is going to say why. **Nobody watched her not write and nobody said one word to her about it, and the chapter says that the not-watching is the whole of what a room can do for a person and that nobody in this city has put it in a form.** She is one of the four, she said yes in her own words on day 330, and **nobody has been asked whether it will go and she has not been asked and is not going to be before the room on day 375.** She may not be made to start waiting to be told what she is for.

**THE TWO WOMEN OF TWENTY-NINE WERE NEVER IN ONE ROOM, AND THE CONSENT FRACTURE IS VISIBLE IN ONE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND IS NOT MENDED IN IT.** **THE ARRANGEMENT THAT HOLDS IT IS ON A PAGE IN CHAPTER 0358 AND WAS PUT THERE BY A REVIEW REPAIR, because the chapter as written had the register-keeper inside the room from the second hour to the fourth with Tamsin Quill in the room all day, and it printed a look at her.** The register-keeper now comes up to the step at the back with no name on it at about the tenth hour of the day with the slate under her arm, looks at four places on a form from the step, is asked about the Saturday from the step, answers the man of about thirty-eight from the step, and goes back down at about the fourth hour of the afternoon. Tamsin Quill is at the bench with the folded sheet of road in her apron from the sixth hour of the morning until the room empties at the ninth hour. **The two of them were about four paces apart for as long as she was there and neither of them said one word to the other at any point in it, and the register-keeper did not look at her once on her way out.** **A previous edition of this block put Tamsin Quill in the room from about the fourth hour to about the ninth and said that one of the two went down a stair about a minute before the other came up it; no chapter supports that near-miss, it was invented by a record describing a scene that is not on the page, and it is withdrawn. Nothing in this volume settles whether these two women are one woman and nothing may.** The fifth condition is not given and is not hinted at, nobody apologises, nobody forgives, nobody refuses an apology, nobody asks for it to be forgiven, and the consent fracture is visible in the same chapter as a space where a man is not and is not mended. Her second handle is a folded sheet of road with about nine places on it, folded the same way every time, which she did not offer to anybody.

**AND THE PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT IN VOLUME 08 AND WERE NOT PUT IN IT BY THIS BATCH.** The keeper of the Redroot gate, whose days are not counted and whose year is not named. The girl of seventeen, who is not fetched. The man of about thirty-seven in `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0293.md`, who is a real person on a page and is not the board-trader. Hettis Roke, forty-four. A woman of about forty-eight in a fourth house at Nine Elms. Jonah Pike. The man of about thirty-two on the bank. The Regent. Ivenn Marrow, whose placement in Volume 08 is a debt and not a page. **And the man of about fifty-nine, who walks the four-hundred-mile road with a barrow and has walked it four times in nine years, is NOT in any of those ten chapters. THIS BLOCK ALSO SAID HE WAS NAMED IN CHAPTER 0360 AS A FACT ABOUT WHAT NOBODY IN THIS CITY HAS A PAGE FOR, AND THAT WAS FALSE: measured on the files, the string returns nothing across Chapters 0351 to 0360 and no chapter of Volume 07 names him at all. It is corrected here in place and the correction is named at the Batch 0002 block at the foot of this file, because a silent correction is the third version of the same fault, and because a file that forbids a later writer from doing something that was never on a page is worse than a file that forgets it. Chapter 0361 is his.**

**AND NOBODY IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS GOT STRONGER.** Adrian Vale is Stage 2 on all ten days, performed no working, opened no threshold, did not name the other world once, was offered no workway and asked for none, is aged nowhere, and **the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across the ten files.** He is named in three of the ten — 0352, 0355 and 0360 — and in no chapter in any form in the other seven, and in all three he is in a room reading a page. **The gains of these ten days are a form on a table, four places read out in the order they are printed, a fourth place named and refused out loud in about four seconds, a man asked what a column is for and answering, a slate with a day on it and nothing under it, a fence finished at sixteen posts and eleven withies, a chalk that stayed in a pocket, a sheet under a stone at a well, and a man who did not stop at a gate. None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.**

It writes Chapters 0361 to 0370, days 361 to 370, and it is told that a batch does not decide what the volume is, does not invent a person, does not answer any of the questions above, and does not record in any file it produces the words the man of about thirty-eight speaks in Chapter 0357.**

# VOLUME 08, BATCH 0002

**This block wins over everything above it. This file records what a person may not do, so a claim in it that is not on a page is a prohibition invented out of nothing, and one such claim in the Batch 0001 block above is withdrawn and named here. Where this file and a chapter disagree about a person, a descriptor, a day or a figure, the chapter is right.** Its record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0002.md` and its ten chapters are on a page.

**AND THE ONE WITHDRAWN CLAIM, WHICH IS A CLAIM ABOUT A PERSON.** The Batch 0001 block above, and the prompt this batch inherited, said that **Chapter 0360 named a man of about fifty-nine as a fact about what nobody in this city has a page for. No chapter of Chapter 0351 to 0360 names him and no chapter of Volume 07 names him at all, and the claim is withdrawn in full.** What is on a page is four chapters later and is below. **A file that forbids a later writer from doing something that was never on a page is worse than a file that forgets it, because the later writer will obey it and will not know she is obeying nothing.**

**WHAT IS NEW ON A PAGE IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS, WITH THE AGE, THE TRADE AND THE SECOND HANDLE BESIDE EACH AND NEVER INSTEAD OF IT.**

**A man of about fifty-nine, who walks a four-hundred-mile road with a barrow and has walked it four times in nine years.** He came up that road about nine years back and found the bar on a gate at the head of it gone through at the bottom the way an ash bar goes through, cut a new one to the old one's pattern, and hung a hurdle on it. **He has made four hurdles in nine years and hung two, and this is the second he has hung there.** His second handle, invented on the page in Chapter 0361, is **a hurdle he made and a bar he replaced**, and it is the same in Chapter 0370, which is the only other chapter he is in. **He is on a page in Volume 08 at last and was on no page in seven volumes, and no chapter of Volume 08 asks him what the four feet is off and no chapter asks him about the gate, and Chapter 0361 says so out loud and Chapter 0370 says it again.** He said one true thing in Chapter 0361, which is that he has walked that road four times in nine years and has never once seen anybody on it, and one in Chapter 0370, which is that a man who has been sent for is a man who has been sent for and after that nobody standing at the far end of anything can find out whether he went or not. **Nobody thanked him for either and he did not ask.**

**The woman of about twenty-one, who has a pen with the nib ground narrow and cut down with a knife.** She walked nine places in three days and wrote nothing on the ninth of them. **Chapter 0363: she was asked which of the four places under a heading is a person and she answered, none of the four, the second one counts them, and nobody in that room marked the moment, and she was not thanked and was not asked a second question and the pen went back into her apron with nothing on it.** She may not be made to start waiting to be told what she is for, and Chapter 0363 does not do it and Chapter 0367 does not either.

**The man of about forty-eight, who has a folding knife with a hinge that does not spring.** He opens it and shuts it twice with the thumb of his other hand to make the joint take, and then it holds about nine minutes, and he has never mentioned it. **He fixed the catch of a window in this city in a room with a date on the door about a week before the forty-second day of that month and about nine people know it and about nine of them are in the rooms he is in.** Chapter 0367 is the chapter where a woman of about twenty-one's pen caught in the grain and asked him two words, and he admitted in one sentence that he ground the first inch and a half of it on a step outside a room in about a quarter of an hour, and said that he was not saying it because he had been asked rather than because he had offered it, and she did not thank him. **His second handle is that nib, and no chapter after 0367 has said it and no chapter may improve on the two words she said about it.**

**The man of about fifty-two who digs, whose name is Nev Cutler, fifty-two, and it says fifty-two in the hand, and the trade it gives is digging, which is the word this room has been using for a man with a spade for a month and is the wrong word and is the only one there is.** His rod is a length of ash with a flat blade on the end of it, ground down on one side and not the other, and it is standing on its end in the corner of a hut about eleven miles the other way where the wall is thickest, and **no chapter of this batch put a rod in anything and no chapter of this batch had him put a hand on anything.** Chapter 0366: he was asked a task, he said yes, and he gave the condition before anybody put one on him — **he will walk it from the near end to the stone and back and say whether a man at the other end would meet him, and he will not put his hands on it, and he will not put a rod in it, and he will not leave a mark of any kind on it, not a stake and not a heap of stone and not a thing he could be found by** — and the room accepted it in about four seconds and he did not offer a day and was not asked for one. Chapter 0370: the task is what the room does, he is not in the room, and about nine people agreed in about four seconds not to send for him, and he has not been told. **He is not asked whether it will go and is not asked to go.**

**The man of about thirty-four who keeps a stall, whose piece of chalk has been in an inside breast pocket since the forty-third day of the Short Month and has not been used once.** Chapter 0362: the woman of about thirty-four beside him lost her voice before the light, and **he said her number for her about nine times over the morning and her voice came back at about the second hour of the afternoon and she said it herself and he stopped in the middle of a number.** He asked in advance not to be thanked and was not thanked and does not mention it. Chapter 0365: he stood in front of a bare wall with the shape of nine boards on it for a morning and did not write on it, and he said out loud that the only two things anybody in four hundred miles would put on a page about that morning are a day and a number of boards, and that neither of those is going on anything. **He is the fourth person in this flood to be refused a thing by somebody who will not be thanked, and he is not thanked and does not say what any of it is worth.**

**The woman of about thirty-four at the stall nearest the water end, who has said about four hundred and forty out loud at the head of her own stall every morning since the thirty-ninth day of that month.** She could not make a sound on the forty-seventh day of that month, and a man beside her said it for her, and she got it back in the afternoon and said it and he stopped. **Her belief that the day she stopped was the day it went came out wrong that day and she has not said so and is not going to, and a chapter may not use it and may not improve on it.**

**The man of about thirty-eight at the wharf, with a tally-board, a knife and a barrow.** He worked a fifth barrow load up onto a stack that cannot be got off the wharf by anybody in Chapter 0368, and was not asked one question by anybody, and in Chapter 0369 he said in a room that he fills the population in four times a year out of what he is given, and that is the first thing he has said in a room since the forty-second day of that month and nobody asked him for it. **He has not been asked one thing since the forty-second day of that month and has not wanted to be, and the figure he has in his head as against the figure he sends is on no page and is printed in none of these ten chapters and in no file this batch produced.**

**The man of about thirty-nine, with a board of nineteen rates.** In Chapter 0368 he put the board down flat on a wet board at the bottom of the river, which nobody in this city has ever seen him do, and a man of about thirty-eight beside a barrow said out loud that a share with the post taken out has been in the inside of a coat in this city since the thirteenth day of the Long Month, which is the hundred and sixty-eighth day of this flood, and that this is the fifty-third day of the Bare Month, and that that is exactly two hundred days. **THE PAPER IS NOT HIS, AND HE SAYS SO BY ASKING ONE QUESTION — *you know what is in it* — AND HE IS TOLD HOW LONG IT HAS BEEN THERE AND NOT HOW HE KNOWS, AND HE ASKS FOR NOTHING. He does not answer it and the man beside the barrow does not look up, and the man the paper belongs to is not in that chapter and is in no chapter of this batch in any form, and has neither said yes nor said no since the day the coat was shut.** He is at the back of four rooms in a fortnight with his slate under his right hand and is not asked in any of them.

**The man of about fifty-seven, with a length of new rope he has not used.** He carried it up a shut road in Chapter 0364, counted the fence out loud, told the man of about sixty with the handcart that sixteen is right, and said out loud, to the fence, that it is the only thing anybody has put up in this flood that is going to be here after everybody who made it. **Nobody asked him where the fence is and nobody told him and he is not defeated and he is right and the rope is still new.**

**The woman of about fifty at the shut gate, with a brass key on a tarred cord.** Chapter 0365: she wrote the fiftieth on the post and rubbed nothing out, gave the whole of a roll in four sentences to nine feet of stone rather than to a man, and refused to tell a man where two bars are and refused to be told where they are. **Nobody asked her anything and nobody thanked her and the two of those are the arrangement.**

**The man of about thirty-one who carries things for a living, with a satchel with a flap.** He has carried nine things in this flood and opened none. In Chapter 0361 he walked out past the last named house with nothing in his hands and stood about nine feet off a man making a hurdle and did not ask him one question, and in Chapter 0370 about nine people in a room worked out that he has been out past the last named house and has come back and said nothing about it, and the not-saying has now got a man into a room that has never been in one. **His not-saying is not a courtesy and is not a mood, and no chapter of this volume may have him open one of the six sheets or the form.**

**The woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register, with her second slate.** She is at the table in Chapters 0362, 0363, 0365, 0366, 0369 and 0370, she wrote the notice for the room on the fifty-first day of that month on the forty-seventh and the notice for the room on the fifty-fifth on the fifty-first, and both are four days, unsigned, with nothing at the top of either, to a place and not to a house. **She wrote the day on the second slate in chalk in front of about nine people four times inside about a month and put nothing under the day on any of the four, and the third column of the register is still empty on purpose and she has not been asked to fill it and did not.**

**Tamsin Quill is in no chapter of these ten, in any form, and her folded sheet of road is in no chapter of these ten, and that is the first time in four chapters that has been true and it is not a decision and it is not an arrangement and nobody arranged it.** The two substring matches a search returns in Chapter 0363 are a woman of about twenty-one's own account of walking nine places in three days.

**THE PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT IN VOLUME 08 AND WERE NOT PUT IN IT BY THIS BATCH.** The keeper of the Redroot gate, whose days are not counted and whose year is not named. The girl of seventeen, who is not fetched. The man of about thirty-seven in `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0293.md`, who is a real person on a page and is not the board-trader. Hettis Roke, forty-four. A woman of about forty-eight in a fourth house at Nine Elms. Jonah Pike. The man of about thirty-two on the bank. The Regent. Ivenn Marrow, whose placement in Volume 08 is a debt and not a page. The man of about forty-one in a very good coat, who is in one chapter of this volume and that is Chapter 0355 and not one of these ten.

**AND NOBODY IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS GOT STRONGER.** Adrian Vale is Stage 2 on all ten days, performed no working, opened no threshold, did not name the other world once, was offered no workway and asked for none, is aged nowhere, **the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across the ten files, and he is in no chapter of this batch in any form, which is six of the seven chapters the Volume 08 outline names him in.** The gains of these ten days are a hurdle and a bar at the head of a four-hundred-mile road, a number said by somebody else for four hours, a question about a column answered and not marked, a fence counted out loud by the man standing in front of it, a bare wall losing a shape, a gate with two bars of which one is on no roll, a task and a condition and nothing left behind him, a nib ground on a step, three barrows of which two belong to nobody, a figure of exactly two hundred days in a coat, four hundred and forty people making a thing that cannot leave this city, and a room deciding not to send for one man. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.**

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A BATCH AND NOT AN OUTLINE.** It writes Chapters 0371 to 0380, days 371 to 380, from a Monday to a Wednesday, and it is told that a batch does not decide what the volume is, does not invent a person, does not answer any of the questions above, does not rewrite Chapters 0355, 0356 or 0358, and does not record in any file it produces the words the man of about thirty-eight speaks in Chapter 0357.

# VOLUME 08, BATCH 0002 — REVIEW REPAIR PASS

**This block wins over everything above it. This file records what a person may not do, so a claim in it that is not on a page is a prohibition invented out of nothing, and one of them was found by the review and is corrected in place above and named here. Where this file and a chapter disagree about a person, a descriptor, a day or a figure, the chapter is right.** Its record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-08-batch-0002.md` and its ten chapters are on a page.

**AND THE CLAIM THAT WAS NOT ON A PAGE, WHICH IS ABOUT TWO PEOPLE.** The Batch 0002 block above said that in Chapter 0368 **the man of about thirty-nine** said the age of a share with the post taken out in the inside of his own coat. **He does not. The paper is Adrian Vale's — it was offered to him on the thirteenth day of the Long Month, day 168, it has been in the inside of his coat since, he has taken it out once, on the seventy-first day of that month, and he has neither said yes nor said no — and a state file and a chapter outrank an outline and a card, and this pass checked `bible/power-system.md` §16.9 and found the paper listed among the things Adrian holds.** The Batch 0002 entry is corrected in place above. **The figure is now said by the man of about thirty-eight who keeps a tally, about a man in this city who is not on those boards, and the man of about thirty-nine asks him one question and is told how long it has been there and not how he knows.** **ADRIAN VALE IS IN NO CHAPTER OF DAYS 361 TO 370 IN ANY FORM, AND HE IS NOT NAMED IN ANY OF THEM, AND THE CHAPTER THAT NAMES THE FIGURE NAMES A MAN INSTEAD AND DOES NOT GIVE HIS NAME, AND THAT IS CORRECT AND MAY NOT BE CHANGED INTO A NAMING.**

**AND THE THREE OTHER FIGURES IN THE BLOCK ABOVE THAT MOVED, none of which is about a person.** The man of about thirty-four's piece of chalk in the Batch 0002 block is described once in the batch's own words and re-run in three shapes rather than six; the man of about thirty-eight is on the boards of the wharf working in Chapter 0368 and is not asked anything in it, and in Chapter 0369 he says in a room that he fills the population in four times a year out of what he is given, and that is the first thing he has said in a room since the forty-second day of that month and nobody asked him for it; and the woman of about thirty-four at the stall nearest the water end lost her voice on the forty-seventh day of that month and got it back the same afternoon, and the number did not go, and her belief that the day she stopped was the day it went came out wrong and she has not said so and a chapter may not use it.

**AND WHAT DID NOT MOVE IN ANY PERSON.** No age moved and no descriptor moved and no trade moved and no second handle moved and no person was invented and no person was removed. **The man of about fifty-nine is still a man who walks a four-hundred-mile road four times in nine years with a barrow and a hurdle he made and a bar he replaced, and he is in two chapters of this batch and no other, and nobody asked him about the gate in either.** The man of about thirty-eight still speaks once, in seven words, on the forty-second day of that month, and what he said is still on a page in Chapter 0357 and in no file, **including this one.** The woman of about fifty-two is still on a page from Chapter 0356 with a door she is not asked to open. The man of about thirty-four still keeps the chalk in the pocket and still is not thanked. The man of about thirty-one still carried nine things and opened none. The man of about twenty-seven still washes the second board nine miles down a road. The man of about sixty still finished a fence at sixteen posts and was asked nothing. The woman of about twenty-one is still in Chapters 0363 and 0367, still asked nothing about the ninth of nine places, and still has a pen with the nib ground narrow that the man of about forty-eight ground. Nev Cutler is still a man of about fifty-two who digs, still fifty-two, still with a rod he does not carry, and now **he has a task with a condition on it and he is not in the room on the fifty-fifth day of that month and nobody sent for him.** **Nobody in these ten chapters got stronger and the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero and the protagonist's name is at zero in all ten files.**

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A BATCH AND NOT AN OUTLINE.** It writes Chapters 0371 to 0380, days 371 to 380, from a Monday to a Wednesday, and it is told that a batch does not decide what the volume is, does not invent a person, does not answer any of the questions above, does not rewrite Chapters 0355, 0356, 0358 or any chapter of 0361 to 0370, and does not record in any file it produces the words the man of about thirty-eight speaks in Chapter 0357.
