# VOLUME 10, BATCH 0005 — THE REVIEW REPAIR, WHICH CORRECTED TWO FIGURES IN THE BLOCK ABOVE AND GIVES THE PROTAGONIST AN INSIDE IN THE THREE CHAPTERS HE IS IN

**ADRIAN VALE — Stage 2, in 0482, 0485 and 0488 and in no chapter in which he reads a page, and in 0491, 0494 and 0496, and after this repair in all three of those with a thought on the page, and the stage and the fifteen and the rule about his hands are all unchanged.** The review's finding is the one this block answers: a man who acts in three chapters and is never once shown deciding anything from the inside is a set of hands and not a character, and a reader who watches fifteen chapters of a man not being useful is entitled to know what the not-being-useful is for.

- **In Chapter 0491 he wanted an order of operations and got the order he wanted.** The string first, the stone second, the wood last, and the wood put in at the corner **where a hand has to go under the board to find it.** He had known before he touched the string that nobody would ask him about it, and his reason for doing it anyway is the order and not a purpose: what he wanted was the doing of it in that sequence and there is nobody in that market to hand the sequence to. The piece of chalk in his inside breast pocket still does not come out and this chapter is still not the chapter that takes it out, and no later chapter may say it is.
- **In Chapter 0494 he could not read a stack for two mornings and read it when the wind came round onto the near end of it.** After that it was in his hands and not in his head. **He was wet to the chest for it and no further forward than he had been the morning before**, which is the cost, and it is a cost of his own body and not of anybody else's day, and nobody on those boards ever learns that he was the one who worked it out. Nobody asked him anything, nobody waited on him to be any use, and the chapter does not say he was right.
- **In Chapter 0496 he brought a plank because a plank is the answer to about nine feet of wet silt and because there was nothing else left in that stack, and that was the whole of the reason and he had it worked out before he got to the near lip.** The wind was doing enough to it to make him look busy and was not doing any of it on purpose. **At the end of it the palm of his hand was wet through and the silt had got into the two lines of it across the middle of the palm, and he wiped it down the front of his coat and did not look at it again that day.** The man of about thirty-one's own account of carrying four things up a four-hundred-mile road and opening none of them is his and is not Adrian's, and the two men are not joined by this repair.

**WHAT DID NOT CHANGE ABOUT HIM. He is in fifteen chapters of fifty — 0453, 0454, 0458, 0461, 0464, 0466, 0471, 0476, 0479, 0482, 0485, 0488, 0491, 0494 and 0496 — three in each movement of ten, and he is in no chapter in which he reads a page, and he is aged nowhere. He gets no stronger, he opens no threshold, no workway is offered to him and he asks for none, and in none of the three does anybody ask him anything, decline to ask him, or stand about waiting on him to be useful. The words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across the ten and *System*, *stat*, *points*, *mana* and *skill* are at zero and *dozen* is at zero, and none of that moved. The restricted first-person count is zero in all ten and every narration paragraph in the three is in the third person, so the inside of him is rendered as what he does and what it costs his body and hands and not as a man talking to himself.**

**THE TWO FIGURES CORRECTED IN THE BLOCK ABOVE, PUBLISHED BESIDE WHAT THEY REPLACE.** *level* is at ONE across this batch's ten files, in Chapter 0491, and at **FIVE in FOUR of the forty-nine** — the other four being Chapter 0476 and Chapter 0479 of water standing or going level in a trough, and Chapter 0483 of two sides of a sheet laid level with each other, which is not water at all and is the one a later writer is most likely to be quoted at. *rooms* is at ONE across the ten, in Chapter 0493, and at **TWO in TWO of the forty-nine**, the other in Chapter 0458, and both are general references and neither is a room count for anybody. *nine people* is at ZERO as a bare cardinal across the ten, and Chapter 0500 contains neither *nine people* nor *about nine people* at all. **And the bolded share, per file 0491 to 0500, is now 16.53, 26.54, 26.57, 14.40, 16.71, 12.27, 25.96, 12.16, 25.41 and 20.48, a range of 12.16 to 26.57, an unweighted mean of 19.70 and a weighted mean of 20.11 on 9,937 words in 150 paragraphs, against 18.19, 26.54, 26.57, 15.99, 16.71, 14.04, 25.96, 12.16, 25.41 and 20.48 and a range of 12.16 to 26.54 and 20.21 and 20.79 and 9,612 and 149 in the block above; the high end there is wrong independently of the repair, because that block's own per-file list gives 26.57 for Chapter 0493 and 26.57 is the high end. The three that moved are 0491, 0494 and 0496 and they moved because three pages grew and the added words are narration and not speech.**

**NO DESCRIPTOR CAME WITH ANY OF IT. The pool is unchanged: the repair took no descriptor for anybody, gave none to anybody, and put no name and no place on a page by narration. The descriptor map on these ten files still returns five descriptors and twenty occurrences and no sixth, and the fourteen incidental figures are still fourteen and not one of them is a new person. The fourteen debts and the twelve routed figures carry forward whole and unpaid, none taken, none repaired and none rerouted by this repair, and the eleven inherited debts are untouched.**

**A close re-measures the cast and does not invent a person and does not attribute a figure to anybody. The instrument is published at `state/volume-10-close.md` §7.9 and it validates itself against Volume 09's published map at fifteen of fifteen on every cell before it is used.**

The two rows in bold are the two that are not in Volume 09's map, and both are people the plan's own cast list carries, and one of the fifteen is a possessive form and is an artefact of the instrument and is named here rather than left in a table. A MAP THAT SHRINKS IS A MAP MEASURING A VOLUME WITH FEWER PEOPLE IN IT: this volume has one new person in it, no notices in it, and an instrument that is a page read out to the nine who were already in the room.**

## 2. THE POOL FOR A NEW PERSON, RE-MEASURED ON ALL FOUR HUNDRED AND NINETY-NINE CHAPTER FILES, AND IT IS ZERO

**Eighty-five distinct descriptors stand across all 499 chapter files. Applying the Volume 10 plan's own rule — a descriptor is available to a new person only if it stands at three occurrences or fewer across every chapter file, every one of those occurrences is a passing mention and not a scene, and it is at zero in each of the last three volumes' fifty — RETURNS ZERO. Volume 10 added no new person, took no descriptor for anybody, invented nothing, and drew no person by narration.**

**AND THE ONE PERSON THIS VOLUME PUT ON A PAGE PROPERLY IS A PERSON WHO WAS ALREADY ON ONE AND WHO HAS NO AGE AND NO DESCRIPTOR ON ANY PAGE IN THIS MANUSCRIPT, and she was put there by being asked, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, on the hundred and thirty-eighth day of the Bare Month, and she answers correctly and goes on working the morning, and she is on a page in 0453 and 0459 and 0498 and is given nothing and asked nothing, and nobody asks her why she stopped. THE NEXT VOLUME THAT NEEDS A PERSON HAS THE SAME PROBLEM AND THE PROBLEM IS NAMED AND NOT SOLVED: a new person in this book will have to arrive without a number, and a writer who cannot see how to do that has to say so in its own outline rather than quietly attach a descriptor to somebody who already has one.**

## 3. WHO IS IN THE FIFTY AND WHO IS IN NONE OF THEM

**ON A PAGE, WITH A DESCRIPTOR, FIFTEEN DESCRIPTORS AND NO MORE.** **THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WHO WASHES THE BOARD: twenty occurrences in ten of the forty-nine, and he is the only person in this manuscript who speaks a volume's name and refuses that volume's one rule, four days apart, at his own cost, and no chapter connects the two acts and he is thanked for neither.** **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER: thirty-three occurrences in twenty-four of the forty-nine, the largest single figure in the volume, and she reads the one-place sheet out, decides the midpoint on a day that is not on a page, asks the question that produces the volume's name, enters the register's third line, and puts the third figure on the board, and she is thanked by nobody in any of the four.**

**AT ZERO ACROSS THE FORTY-NINE AND CARRYING FORWARD UNPAID: the man of about fifty and his second-name form, the man of about forty-five, the man of about thirty-five, the man of about thirty-six, the man of about thirty-three, the man of about thirty, the man of about twenty-five, the man of about twenty-nine, the man of about thirty-two, the man of about twenty, the man of about twenty-two, the man of about twenty-four, the man of about twenty-seven in any other form, the man of about forty-four, the man of about forty-eight, the man of about forty-three, the man of about thirty-six, the man of about forty-two, the man of about forty-seven, the man of about forty-nine, the man of about fifty-two, the man of about fifty-five, the man of about fifty-eight, the man of about fifty-one, the man of about sixty-one, the man of about sixty-two, the man of about sixty-five, the man of about sixty-eight, the man of about sixty-nine, the man of about seventy, the woman of about nineteen, the woman of about twenty, the woman of about twenty-one, the woman of about twenty-two, the woman of about twenty-four, the woman of about twenty-six, the woman of about twenty-seven, the woman of about twenty-eight, the woman of about thirty, the woman of about thirty-one, the woman of about thirty-two, the woman of about thirty-three, the woman of about thirty-four, the woman of about thirty-five, the woman of about thirty-six, the woman of about thirty-seven, the woman of about thirty-eight, the woman of about thirty-nine, the woman of about forty, the woman of about forty-three, the woman of about forty-four, the woman of about forty-five, the woman of about forty-six, the woman of about forty-nine, the woman of about fifty, the woman of about fifty-one, the woman of about fifty-three, the woman of about fifty-four, the woman of about fifty-six, the woman of about fifty-eight, the woman of about fifty-nine, the woman of about sixty, the woman of about sixty-one, the woman of about sixty-five, the woman of about seventy, the girl of about fifteen, the girl of about seventeen, the boy of about eight, the boy of about nine, the boy of about ten, the boy of about eleven, the boy of about fifteen, the person of about twenty-one, Tamsin Quill, the Regent, and the keeper of the Redroot gate.**

**AND THE FIGURE *ABOUT TWO HUNDRED ROOMS* IS NOT AVAILABLE TO A NEW PERSON AND ITS ATTRIBUTION IS UNPAID, re-measured on 499: twenty occurrences in sixteen files, ten in six files of Volume 08 exactly as the Volume 09 close published them, and TEN IN TEN DISTINCT FILES OF VOLUME 07 THAT THE PUBLISHED ROW DID NOT NAME, at 0305, 0311, 0313, 0324, 0325, 0335, 0336, 0343, 0346 and 0349. It is at ZERO in Volumes 01 to 06, in all fifty of Volume 09 and in all forty-nine of Volume 10, and this close carries the debt and attributes it to nobody and does not repair it.**

## 4. ADRIAN VALE, and the one clause the next volume adds to the rule about him

**Stage 2 on all fifty days, in fifteen chapters, and in no chapter in which he reads a page, and with no quoted word in any of the fifteen, and aged nowhere, and not in Chapter 0455, not in Chapter 0490, and not in Chapter 0475 because that chapter is not on disk and because the plan says in its own words that he is not in it. The fifteen are 0453, 0454, 0458, 0461, 0464, 0466, 0471, 0476, 0479, 0482, 0485, 0488, 0491, 0494 and 0496, and they reproduce at fifteen of fifteen on all forty-nine files.**

**ON TEN OF THE FIFTEEN THE PAGE SAYS WHAT HIS HANDS ARE ON AND WHAT HAPPENED TO SOMEBODY ELSE BECAUSE OF THEM. ON FIVE IT SAYS WHAT HE WANTED: in 0461 he is given a barrow he could not read and the narrator's voice says he could see there was one number on it and could not have said what any part of it was a count of, and did not try, and what he had instead was the edge of a tally-board; in 0491 he wanted an order of operations and got it and put the wood where a hand has to go under the board to find it; in 0494 he could not read a stack for two mornings and read it when the wind came round onto the near end of it and was wet to the chest for it and no further forward than the morning before; in 0496 he brought a plank because a plank is the answer to about nine feet of wet silt and because there was nothing else left in the stack, and the palm of his hand was wet through with the silt in the two lines of it.**

**THE NEXT VOLUME'S RULE, AND IT IS ONE CLAUSE LONGER THAN THE LAST: THE RULE ABOUT HIS HANDS STAYS, AND A CARD MAY NOT PUT HIM IN A CHAPTER UNLESS THE CARD NAMES THE THING HIS HANDS ARE ON AND WHAT HE WANTED THE THING FOR, AND A CARD THAT CANNOT NAME THE SECOND MAY USE HIM ONCE IN A BATCH AND NOT TWICE. Nobody in Volume 10 got stronger, he performs no working, no threshold is opened, the other world is not named once across the forty-nine, nobody offers him a workway and he asks for none, and he is in a chapter in which he is the person with the document in none of the fifteen.**

## 5. What a close did not do to any of them

**No person was invented. No name and no place arrived by narration. No descriptor was taken for anybody. No age was put on a woman who has none. No figure was attributed to a person across two finished volumes. No debt was taken, repaired or rerouted. No card was edited and no chapter was moved.**

## VOLUME 11, BATCH 0001 — Chapters 0501 to 0510, days 501 to 510, and the cast of the first ten days of this volume

**This block wins over every block above it. The batch that wrote it is `workspace/volume-11/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` and its record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-11-batch-0001.md`. Where this file and a chapter disagree about a day, a name, a descriptor or a figure, the chapter is right. NO PERSON WAS INVENTED BY THIS BATCH AND NO DESCRIPTOR WAS TAKEN FOR ANYBODY: eight distinct descriptors stand across its ten files and every one of them was already standing on a page before this batch began, and the instrument that measured them reproduces Volume 09's own published map at fourteen of fifteen.**

**ADRIAN VALE. IN THREE OF THE TEN DAYS AND IN NO OTHERS, and the batch's own brief said two and its own day map, its own wants table and its own ninth card all said three; the disagreement is published in the batch record and nobody resolved it. 0501, 0504 and 0509, two occurrences of his name in 0501, one in 0504, two in 0509, and NO SIXTEENTH IS ADDED AND NO CHAPTER IS MOVED. Stage 2 on all ten days. He is aged nowhere. He reads no page and has no quoted word in any of the three and the count of marked turns in which his name appears in the text of the turn is ZERO. He performs no working, no threshold is opened, the other world is not named once, nobody offers him a workway and he asks for none, and *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across the ten files.**

**WHAT HE DID, WHAT HE WANTED, AND WHAT HE CAUSED, in the three chapters. In 0501 his hands were on the store's board, the trough, the rag, the string and the second pass of the wash, and he wanted the second pass to be the last thing he does before the light comes off the flags because the light on the wall above that board is the only hour of the day he does not have to look at the figures on it; he wrung the rag out a second time, the water went under a trestle two stalls along, and the man of about thirty-four had to lift that trestle and set it down a foot further along the row, and about four people saw it move and worked out what put the water there and not one of them said so. In 0504 his hands were on the flap of the satchel, the strap and the near corner of the form, and he wanted that sheet to reach the table with its one place still showing empty and it did and he did not ask what the sheet was for and the reason he did not ask is that a man who asks what he is carrying is a man who has opened it; he set the sheet square with the empty place facing the low side of the room and about four people saw the place was empty before anybody said a word and one of them got up off a bench to look into it and he was not thanked. In 0509 his hands were on the ledge under the second slate and the front edge of the shelf and nothing else at all, and he wanted something flat to go under the far edge of that slate so that it would stop walking, and there was nothing flat within reach of the ledge and he did not go and get anything and he put nothing under it; he held the shelf where it was when it came forward on its two brackets and a man got his eye to the gap and said out loud, in about nine words, that the one place on a form is the shape of the second column in the register-keeper's book, and nobody thanked the man and nobody said whether it was so and nobody in that room knew a man was on the floor. THE WANT IS ON A PAGE IN ALL THREE CHAPTERS, IN THE NARRATOR'S OWN VOICE AND IN THE THIRD PERSON, AND THAT IS THE CLAUSE `outline/volume-11.md` §4.1 ADDS TO THE RULE ABOUT HIM AND IT IS SATISFIED THREE TIMES OUT OF THREE.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER. In six of the ten days: 0502, 0503, 0504, 0505, 0506 and 0510, ten occurrences of her descriptor in seven files. She is the largest figure in the batch. She took the register off its shelf and put it back and wrote nothing in it. She unlocked a chest under that shelf, found a sheet with a heading and one place under it and a figure in that place, said what it was in about nine words before anybody in the room had looked at it, and put the sheet on the shelf above where the register stands. She read the second one-place form out in the local language in daylight with a date in chalk on the door and about nine people in the room and said out loud what it was for before anybody asked, and what it is for is one figure of this city in the one place under that heading to be sent back, and she would not supply the name of the thing it is a count of because the name is not on the page and she was not going to put it there out of her own head. She picked that sheet up at the eighth hour of 0505, turned it so the empty place came out into the room, and carried it to the shelf herself, and she is not the man who carried it up the road. She counted a room out loud twice on 0510 and got two numbers that do not agree and told the room which count she was doing and was told by nobody which one was the count. SHE IS THANKED BY NOBODY IN ANY OF THE TEN DAYS AND SHE IS NOT ASKED ANYTHING IN ANY OF THEM. HER THIRD COLUMN IS EMPTY ON ALL TEN AND SHE DID NOT PUT A PEN IN IT. HER SECOND SLATE IS ON THE SHELF BEHIND HER ON ALL TEN AND IT WAS NOT PICKED UP, NOT TURNED OVER AND NOT WRITTEN ON IN ANY OF THEM, AND ON THE ONE DAY A MAN WAS ON THE FLOOR UNDER IT SHE DID NOT GO NEAR THAT SHELF AT ALL.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WHO WASHES THE STORE'S BOARD. In three of the ten days: 0501, 0502 and 0507, five occurrences of his descriptor. He said four sentences on the first day of this volume about what is on that board, and nobody in that row agreed with him and nobody disagreed with him out loud. He washed that board twice on the hundred and eighty-sixth and the second pass was a thing he wanted and not a thing anybody asked for. On the hundred and ninety-second he lifted the trough, turned it over on the flags and washed the grit out of the bottom of it, and the grit has been coming off that board every morning since the seventh week of this flood and had never been washed out of anything. HE IS NOT ASKED WHY HE DOES NOT WASH THE WALL ABOVE THAT BOARD IN ANY OF THE THREE DAYS AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS VOLUME MAY ASK HIM, AND THE WALL ABOVE THAT BOARD IS NOT WASHED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS. HE HAS SPOKEN THE NAME OF THE VOLUME BEFORE THIS ONE ONCE, ON A DAY THAT IS NOT ONE OF THESE TEN, AND HAS REFUSED THAT VOLUME'S ONE RULE FOUR DAYS LATER, IN THE SAME ROOM, AND NO CHAPTER OF THESE TEN CONNECTS THE TWO ACTS AND NO CHAPTER OF THESE TEN GIVES HIM A LINE AND HE SAYS NOTHING BEYOND HIS FOUR SENTENCES IN ALL THREE OF HIS DAYS.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL. In nine of the ten days, sixteen occurrences of his descriptor, the largest single figure in the batch. A board stands face in against his own trestle and the bare back of it is out in the row, and a piece of chalk of his own has not been out of his inside breast pocket since the two hundred and ninety-eighth day of this flood, which is the forty-third day of the Short Month, and THAT FULL FORM IS PRINTED AT FULL LENGTH IN 0502 AND IN 0509 AND IS SHORTENED TO A RELATIVE PHRASE IN NEITHER. He has a lighter patch on the front of his coat about the size of a hand. He has had to lift a trestle a foot along the row on 0501 because of where somebody else's water went and he did not look at the man who put it there. He said what is wrong with a page in about nine words on 0505 and said he had asked in advance not to be thanked. HE SAID THE SHAPE OUT LOUD ONCE, IN ABOUT NINE WORDS, ON 0509, AND NOBODY THANKED HIM AND NOBODY SAID WHETHER IT WAS SO AND HE DID NOT SAY IT AGAIN.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE WHO TRADES ON A BOARD. In four of the ten days: 0502, 0506, 0508 and 0510, six occurrences. Nineteen rates on the face of it, traded in four markets for nine years, and a space about two fingers wide where a name was in his own hand about nine years ago and is not filled. ON 0502 HE SAID OUT LOUD, IN FRONT OF ABOUT FOUR PEOPLE, THAT HE TOOK IT OFF HIMSELF AND DID NOT SAY WHAT IT WAS, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM, AND HE WENT OUT OF THAT MARKET AT THE END OF THE MORNING WITH THE SPACE IN IT AND NOT ONE PERSON AT EITHER END OF THAT ROW USED HIS NAME. ON 0506 HE SAID HE WOULD NOT PUT THE TWO THINGS SIDE BY SIDE AND THEN SAID HE WAS NOT GOING TO BE THE MAN WHO SAYS TO DO IT. NOBODY IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS ASKED HIM ANYTHING AND THE NAME IS NOT PRINTED IN ANY OF THEM.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO CARRIES THINGS FOR A LIVING. In five of the ten days: 0504, 0505, 0506, 0508 and 0510, eleven occurrences. A satchel with a flap. He came up the road on the quarterly round on the hundred and eighty-ninth with a sheet in it, put the satchel on a table, undid the strap, and said FOUR WORDS: *It is not for me. That is all.* Nobody asked him what was in it and he did not open it and he was not thanked for carrying it. He sat on a bench with the satchel between his two knees through four hours of 0505 and through 0506 and 0508, and he went down the stair at the middle of the day on 0504 carrying less than he had come up with, and nobody at the bottom of that stair asked him what had been in it. HE IS NOT THE MAN WHO CARRIED THE FORM TO THE SHELF ON 0505. EVERYTHING HE HAS CARRIED UP THAT ROAD IN THIS FLOOD IS STILL SHUT AND HE HAS OPENED NONE OF IT AND NOBODY IN THIS FLOOD HAS EVER PUT THE QUESTION TO HIM.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO WHOSE HOUSEHOLD'S DAYS WERE ASKED. In one of the ten days, on 0508, two occurrences of her descriptor. She said four sentences about what she was asked and nothing else, and nobody in that room asked her a second question, and two men at the far end of that bench half turned towards her and did not finish turning, and a man at the door went out of the room and came back and sat in the same place and neither of those two men said one word to her. She was still sitting at the end of that bench at the hour the slate went into the satchel. HER DOOR IS AT THE END OF A LANE PAST TWO GATES AND IT WAS SHUT ALL DAY AND NOBODY IN THIS CITY HAS EVER BEEN ASKED TO OPEN IT. SHE DID NOT SAY THE COUNT-OF-PLACES-AND-COUNT-OF-PEOPLE THING IN A SECOND PLACE AND SHE WAS NOT ASKED TO, AND THE FINDING THAT A HOUSEHOLD'S DAYS ARE NOT A ROTA IS STILL HERS.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN. In one of the ten days, on 0508, one occurrence. A length of new rope over the back of his chair, over it every morning of this flood, and it was not cut and it was not taken up and no hand in that room lifted it off that chair and no mouth at that table said one word about it, and he was not asked where the fence is and he was not told anything about it. The rope stands unused at day 510 and it will stand unused at day 550 and no hand in any of the fifty lifts it.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY WITH THE HANDCART. In one of the ten days, on 0507, two occurrences. A handcart standing in a row with its bar down and a coil of rope that has been used and a hazel mallet with its head bound in cord, and neither the rope nor the mallet was in anybody's hand. HE SAID SIXTEEN WILLOW POSTS AND ELEVEN WITHIES OUT LOUD TO A WOMAN WHO HAD NOT ASKED HIM FOR THEM, AND SAID HE WAS NOT GOING TO SAY THEM AGAIN, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM WHY HE WAS IN THAT ROW AND NOBODY HAD SENT HIM. THE FENCE WAS NOT MEASURED IN THAT CHAPTER AND NO CHAPTER OF THESE TEN MEASURES IT AND NO CHAPTER PRINTS A SEVENTEENTH POST OR A TWELFTH WITY.**

**THE WOMAN WHO WALKED A MARKET ON TWO FEET AND HAS STOPPED. IN NONE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS. She has no name, no age, no number and no descriptor on any page of this manuscript and none was given her here, and nobody in these ten days asks her why she stopped, and she is not on a roll in this city and there is no place in anything this city has written down for a person of hers to be put. The first of the volume's four mornings of a count on foot is day 511, which is not one of these ten days, and the four are 511, 515, 517 and 521 and there is no fifth.**

**AND WHAT NOBODY IN THESE TEN DAYS GOT. Nobody got stronger, no person arrived by narration, no descriptor was taken, no person was invented, no notice was written, no room was summoned, and the wall said nothing on all ten days because this volume's one panel is on day 540.**

---

# VOLUME 11, BATCH 0001 — REVIEW REPAIR PASS, and it wins over everything above it

**THE FOUR EDITS THE PASS MADE TO TWO CHAPTER FILES, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM IS A CHANGE TO A SENTENCE AND NOT TO A PERSON. NO DESCRIPTOR WAS TAKEN, NO PERSON WAS INVENTED, NO AGE WAS CHANGED, NO ONE GOT STRONGER, AND THE MAP AT THE HEAD OF THIS FILE IS UNCHANGED AT EIGHT DESCRIPTORS ACROSS THE TEN CHAPTERS WITH NOT ONE OF THEM NEW.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN IS NAMED ONE MORE TIME THAN HE WAS, AND IT IS THE SAME MAN.** Chapter 0507 gave him one section and now gives him two, because the chapter's hours were put back in order and the section that turned the trough over at the eighth hour now stands at the second, with the board washed at the fourth and the man of about sixty in the row from the seventh. The man of about twenty-seven turns the trough over, fills it, and walks the length of the row with his bucket. **HIS DESCRIPTOR COUNT IN THE BATCH RECORD IS 6 AND WAS 5, AND THAT IS A COUNT OF SENTENCES IN A CHAPTER AND NOT A CHANGE TO THE MAN. He is not asked why he emptied the trough by anybody in that row, he had a standing decision about the wall above that board which he does not mention and does not look up at once, and nobody argued with him and nobody improved on what he said.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY IS UNCHANGED AND THE FENCE IS STILL UNTOUCHED.** Sixteen willow posts and eleven withies, said out loud in a marked turn to nobody who had asked for them, a handcart with a coil of used rope and a hazel mallet with its head bound in cord, and neither the rope nor the mallet used that day. The fence is named once in Chapter 0507, is not measured, and no chapter of this volume's first ten prints a seventeenth post. **HIS POSITION IN THE CHAPTER MOVED FROM THE OPENING SECTION TO THE THIRD AND THAT IS AN ORDERING, NOT A CHARACTER CHANGE.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR IS UNCHANGED.** His own board stands face in against his own trestle with the bare back of it out in the row, the piece of chalk of his own is in his own inside breast pocket and does not come out of it, and the board moved a foot along the row in Chapter 0501 and he is not asked about it. The repeated not-noticing clause that stood three times in Chapter 0501 and twice in Chapter 0507 was rewritten in two of the five places, and **the frame is the frame: about four people look, and not one of the nine says.**

**ADRIAN VALE IS IN THREE OF THE TEN AND NO CHAPTER OF THE TEN CHANGED ON THIS PASS.** He has no quoted word in any of the three, he is aged nowhere, the count of marked turns in which his name appears in the text of the turn is zero, and nobody thanked him and nobody told him he was right. **THE ONE THING THAT CHANGED ABOUT HIM IS NOT TRUE OF HIM AT ALL: it is that the record named the wrong document as the reason he is in Chapter 0509. The chapter and the man are exactly as they were.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE, THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN AND THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO WERE NOT TOUCHED AND APPEAR IN NO CHAPTER THE PASS EDITED.** The second slate is on its shelf, was not picked up, was not turned over and was not written on, and the hand that goes near it is the register-keeper's.

---

# VOLUME 11, BATCH 0002, and it wins over everything above it

**THE MAP AT THE HEAD OF THIS FILE IS UNCHANGED BY THIS BATCH AND IS PUBLISHED AGAIN BESIDE IT: EIGHT DESCRIPTORS ACROSS THE TEN CHAPTERS 0511 TO 0520, NOT ONE OF THEM NEW, EVERY ONE OF THEM ALREADY ON A PAGE BEFORE THIS BATCH BEGAN, AND NO PERSON INVENTED AND NO AGE CHANGED AND NOBODY GIVEN A SECOND DESCRIPTOR.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WHO WASHES THE BOARD. In four of the ten chapters, on 0511, 0512, 0514 and 0516, six occurrences. A bucket, a rag, and a wall he has decided not to wash. ON DAY 516 HE SAID OUT LOUD, AT THE SEVENTH HOUR, TO THE TROUGH AND NOT TO THE ROW, ONCE, THAT HE HAS NOT WASHED THAT WALL AND IS NOT GOING TO AND IS NOT GOING TO SAY WHY, AND HE DID NOT SAY WHY, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM WHY, AND NOBODY IMPROVED ON IT. AT THE NINTH HOUR OF THAT SAME EVENING HE PUT HIS HAND FLAT ON THE WALL ABOVE THAT BOARD, HELD IT THERE WHILE THE LIGHT CAME OFF THE FLAGS AND A MAN WENT PAST HIM ON THEM, AND TOOK IT OFF AGAIN WITHOUT A RAG AND WITHOUT WATER. THE RAG WENT INTO THE TROUGH AND DID NOT COME OUT. HIS TROUGH WAS CARRIED FOUR ROWS AWAY ON DAY 512 BY TWO PEOPLE WHILE HE WATCHED AND SAID NOTHING, AND ON DAY 512 HE WASHED THAT BOARD ON THE GROUND AND SAID HE WAS NOT GOING TO EXPLAIN TO ANYBODY WHAT A TROUGH IS FOR, AND ON DAY 514 HE WASHED IT ON THE GROUND AGAIN AND A MAN HE DID NOT ASK CARRYED THE TROUGH BACK THE NEXT MORNING AND HE DID NOT LOOK AT THAT MAN DO IT. HE IS NOT ASKED WHY HE DOES NOT WASH THE WALL IN ANY CHAPTER OF THIS VOLUME AND NO CHAPTER MAY.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT AT THE SALT WHARF. In three of the ten chapters, on 0511, 0517 and 0519, four occurrences, and he is one man in all three. A tally-board, a knife and a barrow. ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED AND FORTY IS ON THAT BOARD IN HIS OWN HAND AND IT IS A FIGURE OF THIS CITY'S OWN OF THE SAME KIND OF THING AS THE COUNT MADE ON FOOT, AND THE FIGURE HE HAS IS ON NO PAGE AND IS PRINTED NOWHERE. ON DAY 511 HE SAID OUT LOAD THAT HE HAS NEVER WALKED THOSE ELEVEN MILES AND WOULD RATHER THAT WAS IN THE RECORD THAN THAT SOMEBODY FOUND OUT ABOUT IT LATER. ON DAY 519 HE LAID THE BOARD FACE DOWN ON A STACK OF BOARDS IN THE OPEN AND TOOK HIS BARROW UP THE ROW AND SAID THAT ANYBODY WHO WANTS TO FIND IT CAN COME AND TURN IT OVER AND THAT NOBODY IN THIS CITY IS GOING TO. ON DAY 517 HE GOT HIS BARROW ACROSS TWO THIRDS OF A CHANNEL ON THE WHEEL OF IT OVER SOMEBODY ELSE'S PLANK AND SAID THAT SOMEBODY HAD DONE HIM A SERVICE AND THAT IT WAS NOT DONE FOR HIM, AND HE DID NOT LOOK AT THE PLANK AND DID NOT LOOK DOWN AT THE FLAGS.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL. In four of the ten chapters, on 0511, 0514, 0516 and 0519, six occurrences. His own board stands face in against his own trestle with the bare back of it out in the row, and his own piece of chalk is in his own inside breast pocket. ON DAY 514 HE SAID OUT LOUD THAT THE CHALK IN THE BOX ON THE STEP IS THE STORE'S CHALK AND IS NOT HIS, THAT HIS OWN HAS BEEN IN THAT POCKET SINCE THE TWO HUNDRED AND NINETY-EIGHTH DAY OF THIS FLOOD, WHICH IS THE FORTY-THIRD DAY OF THE SHORT MONTH, AND THAT IT IS A DIFFERENT CHALK; AND HE DID NOT SHORTEN THAT DATE AND HE DID NOT TAKE THE OTHER ONE. HE ALSO SAID OUT LOUD THAT THERE IS ABOUT FOUR FEET OF STANDING WATER AT THE NEAR END OF THAT ROW WHERE THE CHANNEL HAS CUT DOWN AND THAT NOBODY HAS TAKEN A CORD TO IT AND NOBODY EVER WILL. AND AT THE SEVENTH HOUR HE CARRIED THE TROUGH BACK THE LENGTH OF THAT ROW HIMSELF AND PUT IT UNDER THE BOARD ON ITS OWN SPOT, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM TO AND NOBODY THANKED HIM AND THE MAN WHO WASHES THAT BOARD DID NOT LOOK AT HIM DO IT. ON DAY 519 HE WALKED PAST THE BOX ON THE STEP FOUR TIMES AND DID NOT STOP AT IT.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE WHO TRADES ON A BOARD. In two of the ten chapters, on 0516 and 0518, five occurrences. A board under his arm, nineteen rates, and a space about the width of two fingers where a name was in his own hand about nine years ago and was never filled. ON DAY 518 HE PUT THAT BOARD FACE UP ON A TABLE IN THAT ROOM AND WAS RIGHT IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS ABOUT THE ORDER THE THINGS ON IT STAND IN, AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM SAID HE WAS RIGHT, AND HE SAT DOWN AT THE NEAR END OF THAT BENCH AND SAID NO WORD MORE. THE NAME IS NOT PRINTED IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM ASKED HIM ABOUT IT. ON DAY 516 HE STOOD WITH HIS BACK TO THE WALL ABOVE THAT BOARD AND SAID NOTHING ABOUT IT AND WAS ONE OF THE TWO MEN WHO ASKED HIM NOTHING ABOUT IT.**

**THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER. In two of the ten chapters, on 0518 and 0520, four occurrences, and she is one woman in both and not two. HER SECOND SLATE IS ON THE SHELF BEHIND HER WITH A DAY ACROSS THE HEAD OF IT AND NOTHING UNDER THAT DAY, AND ON DAY 515 IT WAS NOT PICKED UP AND NOT TURNED OVER AND NOT WRITTEN ON AND NO HAND BUT HERS WENT NEAR THAT SHELF. ON DAY 515 SHE WROTE THE SECOND COUNT UNDER THE FIRST ONE ON A WORKING SLATE AND NEITHER WAS STRUCK OUT. ON DAY 520 SHE TOOK THE REGISTER OFF ITS SHELF IN HER OWN TWO HANDS, PUT HER FINGER DOWN THE RULED LINES OF ITS MIDDLE COLUMN AND SAID ONE WORD OUT LOUD, WROTE NOTHING IN EITHER COLUMN, AND PUT IT BACK ON THE SHELF AT THE ELEVENTH HOUR BY THE SAME TWO HANDS.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO CARRIES THINGS FOR A LIVING. In one of the ten chapters, on 0513, three occurrences, and he is one man. A satchel with a flap, the strap round it twice, and nothing in it. HE WENT UP THE SHUT ROAD OUT PAST THE LAST NAMED HOUSE ON THE QUARTERLY ROUND AND STOOD AT THE TOP OF IT AT THE NINTH HOUR WITH HIS HAND ON THE STRAP AND DID NOT GO THROUGH AND WENT BACK DOWN IT, AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT HE WOULD RATHER SOMEBODY WROTE DOWN THAT HE STOOD THERE THAN THAT THEY WROTE DOWN THAT HE WENT THROUGH. NOBODY ASKED HIM ANYTHING AND NOBODY THANKED HIM AND THE COST OF THE REFUSAL THAT IS COMING ON DAY 540 IS HIS OWN SILENCE AND IT IS NOT SPENT.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY. In one of the ten chapters, on 0513, three occurrences. A hazel mallet with its head bound in cord and a coil of rope, and he is at the top of the shut road without his handcart. HE SAID SIXTEEN WILLOW POSTS AND ELEVEN WITHIES OUT LOUD TO A WOMAN WHO HAD NOT ASKED HIM FOR THEM AND SAID THAT WAS EVERYTHING HE HAD GOT TO SAY ABOUT IT IN FRONT OF ANYBODY. THE FENCE WAS NOT MEASURED AND THE CORNER IN IT WAS NOT MEASURED AND THE GAP IN IT WAS NOT MEASURED AND NO POST WAS MOVED AND NO WITHY WAS BOUND AND NOBODY HAS ASKED HIM WHY HE PUT IT THERE.**

**THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN. In one of the ten chapters, on 0520, one occurrence. A length of new rope over the back of his chair, over it every morning of this flood, and it did not move in that room on that morning and no hand lifted it and nobody looked at it once and nobody asked him anything at all. He was not asked where the fence is and he was not told anything about it.**

**ADRIAN VALE IS IN TWO OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND IN THE OTHER EIGHT HE IS NOT NAMED AT ALL. In 0512 his hands are on the flags at the end of that market, the sill of the store's step, and the strap of a tin that is about two hands away from him, and he does not touch the strap and does not touch the tin and does not move the sill; he banks the silt from eleven miles of flats against that step so that the quiet end of that market is bare stone, and the consequence of that is a heap the man of about twenty-seven has to clear off his own step with his own hands and a channel of water the length of that market that the woman with the slate steps over four times at the near gate. In 0517 his hands are on a plank, on the wet sill of a second row of the flats and on both ends of the plank, and he wanted it to reach that sill and it reached about two thirds of the way and he was wet to the chest for it and he was no further forward than the morning before, and the consequence of that is a barrow that got across two thirds of a channel on it and a woman who went over it and came back and put the near end of it back with her foot. HE HAS NO QUOTED WORD IN EITHER CHAPTER, HE IS AGED IN NEITHER, HE READS NO PAGE IN EITHER, NOBODY ASKS HIM ANYTHING IN EITHER, NOBODY THANKS HIM, NOBODY TELLS HIM HE WAS RIGHT, AND NOBODY IN EITHER CHAPTER IS WAITING FOR HIM TO BE USEFUL AND HE IS NOT USEFUL TO ANYBODY IN EITHER.**

**THE WOMAN WHO WALKED A MARKET ON TWO FEET AND HAS STOPPED. IN SEVEN OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND SHE HAS NO NAME, NO AGE, NO NUMBER AND NO DESCRIPTOR ON ANY PAGE AND NONE WAS GIVEN HER HERE. A slate under her arm and a tin in her two hands, both of which are on a page already. She counted the shut doorways of eleven miles of flats on day 511 and wrote a figure and did not say it out loud; she crossed the near gate on day 512 and stepped over four times into the water that came off the flags of the market end; she wrote a second figure on day 515 and it does not agree with the first; she went over two thirds of a channel on day 517 and came back; and on day 519 she spent a morning on eleven miles of flats with a slate and a tin and no figure came out of it. NOBODY IN THESE TEN DAYS ASKS HER ANYTHING, AND NOBODY ASKS HER WHY SHE STOPPED, AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE THE FIRST TO ASK HER WHY A MORNING CAME OUT WITH NOTHING WRITTEN ON IT.**

**AND WHAT NOBODY IN THESE TEN DAYS GOT. Nobody got stronger, no person arrived by narration, no descriptor was taken, no person was invented, no notice was written, no room was summoned, and the wall said nothing on all ten days because this volume's one panel is on day 540.**

---

# VOLUME 11, BATCH 0002 — REVIEW REPAIR PASS

**At the foot of the file and it wins over every block above it. No line above this one was edited. No person was created, no descriptor was taken, and nobody in these ten days got stronger, older, named, thanked or told he was right.**

**1. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WHO WASHES THAT BOARD. THE ONE FIGURE IN THE BATCH WHOSE INTERIOR THIS PASS CHANGED, AND IT CHANGED BY REMOVING A RESTATEMENT.** What is established about him now, and stands: he has washed that board every morning since the seventh week of this flood; his washing stops at the top of the board where the wall begins; the bucket goes in the trough and the rag goes on the stone lip and nothing goes above that line; he has said out loud, unasked, that he has not washed the wall above that board and is not going to and is not going to say why; and at the ninth hour he put his bare hand flat on that wall, with the rag under it on the lip, and took it off again without a rag and without water and without saying anything. **What changed is that he no longer restates the woman before refusing her, and no second person has touched that wall and nobody has asked him why. The chapter's best exchange was left exactly as it was, because it shifts *you* into *me* and that shift is characterisation rather than a tic.**

**2. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL.** Unchanged in what he does and changed in one line of speech: he no longer opens by agreeing that it was a good fence before the water, and no longer opens by agreeing there is nothing up there for the carrier. He says it is a fence now, that nobody put him on that road, and that there was nothing up there for him the other nine years. He still carries his own chalk in his own pocket, still does not take the store's chalk, still says the standing water at the near end has never had a cord taken to it, and still carried the trough back the length of that row unthanked.

**3. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER.** Her answer to *You do not know that* no longer begins by agreeing that she does not know. She goes straight to the slate that goes in and out of the room on a barrow and to the man who carries it not being asked. **Her chapter, 0520, was not touched at all.** The three columns stand, the one on the right is empty from the top of the page to the bottom, the middle one has five lines' worth of room and nothing in it, the second slate was not picked up and not turned over and not written on, and no hand but hers went near that shelf.

**4. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT AT THE SALT WHARF.** His answer no longer confirms what the woman said. He goes straight to the four hundred and forty being on the far side of the board where the sun does not reach, and to nobody in this city turning it over. He still got his barrow across two thirds of a channel on a plank he did not know whose, still did not look at the plank, and still said he would rather it were in the record that somebody had done him a service and not done it for him.

**5. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE WHO TRADES ON A BOARD.** His answer no longer opens by agreeing that it is nineteen rates. He says how long it has been nineteen, and that nobody in that room has ever offered him a second board. He is still right in about four seconds and still is not told so.

**6. ADRIAN VALE. UNCHANGED, AND THE REVIEW THAT TRIGGERED THIS PASS CALLED HIM ABSENT.** He is in 0512 and 0517 and in no other of the ten, has no quoted word in either, is not thanked and is not told he was right, and §14.3 of the volume's plan put him in those two days and no others. **The banked silt and the barrow across the plank are still the whole of what he does, and the question of whether a protagonist in two chapters out of forty is a book is carried to the next outline and answered there and not here.**

**7. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WITH THE RAG.** Not a person; the fault. The rag is on the stone lip of the step and stayed there from the fourth hour to the ninth, and this is now unbroken in all twenty files of the volume.

---

# VOLUME 11, BATCH 0003 — Chapters 0521 to 0530, days 521 to 530

**At the foot of the file and it wins over every block above it. No line above this one was edited. No person was created, no descriptor was taken, and nobody in these ten days got stronger, older, named, thanked or told he was right.**

**1. ADRIAN VALE. IN TWO OF THESE TEN AND IN EIGHT HE IS NOT NAMED AT ALL.** In 0521 his hands are on a stub of the store's chalk, a string, a stone and the four corners of one step, and that stub goes back in the box on that step when he is done with it and there is more of that chalk in the box than he took out of it, and he wanted a figure to come out the same twice and it did not, and he wrote both of them, and **he is the cause of a figure coming off the stone of that step onto the flat of a woman's boot and of a man cutting a length of string off his own barrow's wheel and putting it down, and neither of those is a decision or a figure of this city or a document or a notice, and nobody knows whose hands were on that step, and nobody in that market thanked him, told him he was right, or waited for him to be useful.** In 0529 his hands are on the pen, the ink, the ruled line and the shoulder of the book, and he wanted that figure to go in above the other one and not beside it, and **he is the cause of a man with a board under his arm standing in a doorway for the length of a count and coming in last, and nobody in that room knows why, and nobody asked him anything, and he is not useful to one person in that chapter. HE DID NOT LOOK DOWN INTO THE OPEN PAGE AT ANY HOUR OF THAT MORNING AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM SAW HIM LOOK DOWN INTO IT.** He has no quoted word in either chapter, he is aged in neither, he reads no page in either, and **he is the only person named in this batch who is named at all, and the name appears in two files and in eight paragraphs.**

**2. THE WOMAN WHO WALKED A MARKET ON TWO FEET AND HAS STOPPED.** IN **TWO** OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, 0521 AND 0526, AND SHE HAS NO NAME, NO AGE, NO NUMBER AND NO DESCRIPTOR ON ANY PAGE AND NONE WAS GIVEN HER HERE. **THE FIRST EDITION OF THIS HEADLINE SAID *IN SIX OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS* AND IT IS WITHDRAWN: SHE IS IN TWO AND IN TWO ONLY, and the four extra were a merge of her with the woman of about sixty-nine, who carries the other tin and is item 9 of this list, and on day 528 she is not in the chapter at all and the chapter says so in a plain sentence of narration. NOBODY NAMED HER AND NOBODY ASKED HER ANYTHING ON EITHER OF HER TWO DAYS AND THE FINDING IS STRONGER FOR THE CORRECTION, NOT WEAKER.** A slate under her arm and a tin in her two hands, both on a page already. She counted the shut doorways of eleven miles of flats on day 521 and wrote a fourth figure that does not agree with the other three; she carried one of two figures written in chalk on a stone step off that step on the flat of her boot and eleven miles of flats to a stack of boards under a tarred cloth; she scrubbed a tin out on a stone sill at the near end of those flats on day 526 and carried it up into a room and left a wet ring on a table. **NOBODY IN THESE TEN DAYS ASKS HER ANYTHING, NOBODY ASKS HER WHY SHE STOPPED, AND ON DAY 526 THE CHAPTER SAYS OUT LOUD THAT SHE HAS WALKED THOSE FLATS ON FOUR MORNINGS AND THAT THIS WAS NOT ONE OF THEM.**

**3. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER. THE FIGURE IN THIS BATCH WHOSE INTERIOR MOVED.** She set four figures one under another on a working slate in the order they were made and said the order is the only thing about them anybody can be sure of. **On day 525 she said the cost out loud before anybody had spoken, in her own mouth, in eleven words — only a count made on foot can be shown a person — and then said the rest of it: *THE FIRST EDITION OF THIS LINE SAID *IN ABOUT FIFTEEN WORDS* AND GAVE THE TURN AS *a count made on foot is the only figure anybody can be shown a person*; `outline/volume-11.md` §8 budgets about eleven words and the page overran it, the page is corrected, and the state layer had ratified the overrun. THE REST OF HER TURN, THE BILL, AND THE DECISION ARE UNMOVED.* choosing one of four does not mend the other three, and a person four hundred miles off is going to be handed one of them and is not going to be told there were four.** She decided that all four go into the middle column in the order they were made, that the most recent is the one that goes, and that the column on the right stays empty and is never going to be explained. She did not thank anybody and nobody thanked her. **On day 530 she wrote four lines into that column in the order they were made with the day beside each, the column is ruled into five lines, and that made five figures in five lines and the column is full from that morning and there is no more room in it for anything at all, and she said out loud in that room that there is no room left in it for a sixth figure of this city's own and that the column on the right of that book is empty and is going to stay empty.** *THE FIRST EDITION OF THIS LINE ENDED *AND LEFT ONE LINE OF ROOM*, AND THAT IS WITHDRAWN: five figures in five ruled lines leave no room, the page of Chapter 0530 said otherwise and was corrected, and the first edition of the page is published in the record of that batch.* **She is the one who opened the register and the one who put it back, and her hand went near the shelf exactly once.** The third column is empty and she has never given a reason out loud and nobody in this volume has asked her for one.

**4. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL. TWO MEN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR ARE TWO PEOPLE AND WERE NOT MERGED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS.** His own board is face in against his own trestle with the bare back out in the row, and his own piece of chalk is in his own inside breast pocket and has been since the two hundred and ninety-eighth day of this flood, which is the forty-third day of the Short Month, printed at full length in every chapter of this batch that carries it. **On day 524 he said out loud that neither of the two chalks in this city is going to be used that morning and that nobody in that room is going to ask him for his, and nobody did.** *AND TWO SPEECHES THIS LIST GAVE HIM ARE NOT HIS ON THE PAGE, AND THE FIRST EDITION OF THIS ITEM SAID THEY WERE: it said On day 528 he said that he watched the washer go out of that row with a trough on his shoulder, and On day 521 he said there is writing on that step and it is not on his board and he is not going to ask who has put it there.* **The page prints `chapter-0521.md:35` as *A woman at a stall two along said it out loud, to the flags* and `chapter-0528.md:25` as *A woman two stalls along said the thing that is said, to the flags*, and both of them are the PROMPT of the exchange, and the marked reply that follows each of them carries no lead-in at all, so no page in this batch names who answers either of them. THE ATTRIBUTION TO HIM IS A READING AND IT IS PUBLISHED AS A READING, with its two reasons: he is the only person in that row carrying a board, and he is the only person in that row in a position to have watched the washer go out of it. NO LEAD-IN WAS ADDED TO EITHER CHAPTER AND NO PAGE WAS CHANGED FOR THIS, because a lead-in is not an object row and this batch's own record repaired ten of them for that reason. He was not thanked on any day this batch and was not told he was right on any day this batch.**

**5. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WHO WASHES THAT BOARD.** He washed that board at the fourth hour on day 521 and on day 528 and put the rag on the stone lip both times and did not wash the wall above it on either. **On day 528 he carried the trough, the bucket and the rag eleven miles of flats out of that market and eleven miles of the same road back in weather, and said nine words about the water at the end of that market, and nobody asked him why he went and nobody thanked him and he did not stop to say so, and a stone was in the bottom of that trough when it came back and he did not put his hand in the water to find out where it came from and said nothing to anybody about it.**

**6. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT AT THE SALT WHARF.** He lost a length of string off his own wheel on day 521, cut it off in two places with a knife, put the two pieces and a stone down on the flags, and said one turn about it, and **nobody at that end of the market asked him whose string it was and nobody asked about the chalk on that step.** His own figure is on no page and is printed nowhere and he said nothing about it in this batch.

**7. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE WHO TRADES ON A BOARD.** Nineteen rates, a space about two fingers wide in the bottom of the middle column of his board that is not filled, and no name on it and no question about it. **He was right in about four seconds on day 525 about why the top one of a column is the one that goes, and nobody said he was right. On day 529 he said out loud from a doorway that a wall is read across and a book is read down and that he is not going to be the man who says which one a person is meant to be using. On day 530 he said that a price goes where the next price goes and that that is the whole of what he knows about an order.** Nobody thanked him on any of the three days and nobody improved on him.

**8. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO CARRIES THINGS FOR A LIVING.** In one chapter of these ten, on day 527, and he put the satchel down on a bench and **did not open it**, and he put both hands on the ledge under the second slate and put nothing under it and did not go and get anything and did not send anybody to get anything, and he got grit into the two lines of his palms taking his hands off. **He does not know what is in that satchel and no page of this volume has told him and no chapter of this batch came near telling him.**

**9. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE AND HER TIN.** She came up nine steps on day 523 with an empty tin in her two hands, put it on a table in a room with about nine people in it, and did not ask the fourth question she has not asked since the third of three, and the three of them were about a person, a fence and a line. **One man in that room noticed that she had not asked and said nothing about it and has said nothing since, and nobody else noticed, and the tin is on that table with the lid off and nothing under it and nobody in this batch filled it.**

**10. THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WITH A CHAIR, THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN, AND EVERYBODY ELSE.** The man of about sixty stands at a near gate with a handcart, a coil of rope and a hazel mallet with its head bound in cord, in one paragraph, and is not asked anything. **The man of about fifty-seven and his length of new rope are not named in any of these ten chapters at all and the rope was not lifted because neither of them is in the batch.** The man of about thirty-four carries a chair up a stair and back down it on day 523 and is not thanked for it.

**11. AND WHAT NOBODY IN THESE TEN DAYS GOT.** Nobody got stronger, no person arrived by narration, no descriptor was taken, no person was invented, no notice was written, no room was summoned, and **the wall said nothing on all ten days because this volume's one panel is on day 540 and is unspent.**

# VOLUME 11, BATCH 0003 — REVIEW REPAIR PASS

**This block is at the foot of the file and wins over every block above it. Five lines above this one are corrected in place with their first editions published at the line itself: Adrian Vale's hands on the chalk in 0521, the woman who walked a market's chapter count, the register-keeper's cost in words and her column on day 530, and the two speeches the man of about thirty-four was given that the page puts in a woman's mouth as the prompt. Nothing above this one was deleted and no person was created, named, aged, numbered, described, thanked, or told they were right.**

**1. THE FIVE PEOPLE IN THIS BATCH WHOSE STATE ON A PAGE WAS WRONG AGAINST THE PLAN, AND WHAT IS SETTLED NOW.**

1. **ADRIAN VALE. UNCHANGED, AND THE ONLY THING ABOUT HIM THAT MOVED WAS WHOSE HANDS WERE ON THE STEP.** In 0521 his hands are on **a stub** of the store's chalk, a string, a stone and the four corners of one step, **and that stub goes back in the box when he is done with it because there is more of that chalk in the box than he took out of it.** He wanted a figure to come out the same twice and it did not and he wrote both of them, and he is the cause of a figure coming off that step onto the flat of a woman's boot and of a length of string coming off a barrow's wheel, and nobody in that market knew, and nobody thanked him, and he is not useful to any of them. In 0529 his hands are on the pen, the ink, the shoulder of the book and the ruled line, and **he does not look down into the open page at any hour of that morning**, and he is the cause of a man with a board under his arm standing in a doorway and coming in last, and nobody in that room asked him anything. He is Stage 2 on all ten days, he is in two chapters and in no chapter in which he reads a page, he has no quoted word in either, he is aged nowhere, and the other world is not named once in the ten files. *THE FIRST EDITION OF THE ABOVE SAID *THE LAST OF THE STORE'S CHALK*, WHICH WOULD HAVE MEANT THE BOX ON THAT STEP WAS EMPTY FOR THE REST OF THE BATCH AND FOUR OTHER CHAPTERS SAY IT IS NOT.*
2. **THE WOMAN WHO WALKED A MARKET. IN **TWO** OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, 0521 AND 0526, AND NOT SIX, AND SHE IS STILL THE FINDING.** A slate under her arm and a tin in her two hands, both of them on a page already, and **no name, no age, no number and no descriptor on any page of this manuscript and none given her here.** On 521 she goes up eleven miles of flats, writes a fourth figure that does not agree with the other three, comes back down the row, takes one of two figures written in chalk on a stone step off that step on the flat of her boot and carries it eleven miles to a stack of boards. On 526 she is at the near end of those flats with the tin and no slate, she scrubs the tin out on a stone sill, carries it up into a room and leaves a wet ring on the table. **NOBODY ASKS HER ANYTHING ON EITHER DAY, NOBODY ASKS HER WHY SHE STOPPED, AND ON DAY 526 THE CHAPTER SAYS OUT LOUD THAT SHE HAS WALKED THOSE FLATS ON FOUR MORNINGS AND THAT THIS WAS NOT ONE OF THEM.** She is not in 0523 — the tin on that chapter's table is the woman of about sixty-nine's and she carries no slate — and she is not in 0528 and that chapter says so in a plain sentence of narration. **THE FIRST EDITION OF THE HEADLINE ABOVE SAID SIX CHAPTERS AND NAMED HER ON DAYS 523 AND 528; IT IS WITHDRAWN AND PUBLISHED AT THE LINE. NOTHING ELSE ABOUT HER MOVED AND THE FINDING IS STRONGER, NOT WEAKER, FOR BEING TWO.**
3. **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER. THE FIGURE IN THIS BATCH WHOSE INTERIOR MOVED, AND TWO FIGURES OF HERS ARE NOW SETTLED AGAINST THE PLAN.** On day 525 she said the cost out loud before anybody had spoken, in her own mouth, in eleven words — **only a count made on foot can be shown a person** — and then said the rest of it, which is the bill and is hers: choosing one of four does not mend the other three, and a person four hundred miles off is going to be handed one of them and is not going to be told there were four. **THE FIRST EDITION OF THAT TURN WAS FIFTEEN WORDS — *a count made on foot is the only figure anybody can be shown a person* — AGAINST `outline/volume-11.md` §8's BUDGET OF ABOUT ELEVEN, AND THE FIRST EDITION OF THIS FILE PUBLISHED THE FIFTEEN AND RATIFIED IT. THE PHRASE IS NOW ELEVEN WORDS AND IT IS FIXED, AND NO LATER PHASE OF THIS VOLUME MAY PARAPHRASE IT, IMPROVE ON IT, LENGTHEN IT, SHORTEN IT OR PUT IT IN A SECOND MOUTH.** She decided that all four go into the middle column in the order they were made, that the most recent is the one that goes, and that the column on the right stays empty and is never going to be explained. She did not thank anybody and nobody thanked her. On day 530 **she wrote four figures into a column that `chapter-0520.md:9-11` had her count the ruled lines of and say *Five.* out loud, and that made it full, and she said out loud that there is no room left in it for a sixth figure of this city's own and that the column on the right of that book is empty and is going to stay empty** — and she is the one who opened the register and the one who put it back, and her hand went near that shelf exactly once. **THE FIRST EDITION OF THIS FILE SAID SHE LEFT ONE LINE OF ROOM. FIVE FIGURES IN FIVE RULED LINES LEAVE NO ROOM, `outline/volume-11.md` §20.6 SAYS THE COLUMN HOLDS FIVE FROM DAY 530, AND THE FIRST EDITION IS PUBLISHED AT THE LINE.** The third column is empty and she has never given a reason out loud and nobody in this volume has asked her for one.
4. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL. TWO MEN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR ARE TWO PEOPLE AND WERE NOT MERGED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, AND TWO SPEECHES ARE NOT HIS.** His own board is face in against his own trestle with the bare back out in the row, and his own piece of chalk is in his own inside breast pocket and has been since the two hundred and ninety-eighth day of this flood, which is the forty-third day of the Short Month, printed at full length in every chapter of this batch that carries it. On day 524 he said out loud that neither of the two chalks in this city is going to be used that morning and that nobody in that room is going to ask him for his, and nobody did. **THE TWO SPEECHES THIS FILE PREVIOUSLY GAVE HIM ARE NOT ON THE PAGE AS HIS. `chapter-0521.md:35` reads *A woman at a stall two along said it out loud, to the flags* and `chapter-0528.md:25` reads *A woman two stalls along said the thing that is said, to the flags*, and each of those is the PROMPT of its exchange, and the marked reply that follows each carries no lead-in at all, so no page in this batch names who answers either of them. THE ATTRIBUTION TO HIM IS A READING AND IS PUBLISHED AS A READING: he is the only person in that row carrying a board, and he is the only person in that row in a position to have watched the man who washes it go out of it. NO LEAD-IN WAS ADDED TO EITHER CHAPTER, because a lead-in is not an object row of §20.6 and this batch's own record repaired ten of them for exactly that reason, and a repair pass that undid its own rule to make a state file read better is the failure this file exists to prevent.** He was not thanked on any day of this batch and was not told he was right on any day of it.
5. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO CARRIES THINGS FOR A LIVING. UNCHANGED, AND THE CHAPTER HE IS IN HAD ITS ONE MOVEMENT REPAIRED.** On day 527 he put the satchel down on a bench and did not open it, **put his left hand flat on the ledge under the second slate and got the fingers of it over the front edge of the shelf, the front of that shelf came up under the heel of his hand, the slate ran back down the slope of it and came up against the wall behind it, and he put nothing under it and did not go and get anything and did not send anybody to get anything.** The grit on that ledge went into the two lines of his palms. **The first edition of that chapter said the shelf came forward and the slate came forward with it, and the same chapter then said the slate was a hand's width further back than it was at the seventh hour, and the page is repaired so that the one movement runs one way.** He does not know what is in the satchel and no page of this volume has told him and no chapter of this batch came near telling him. **HE IS NOT ADRIAN VALE AND THE TWO MEN ARE NOT CONNECTED ON ANY PAGE AND NO CHAPTER OF THE FIFTY MAY CONNECT THEM.**

**2. WHAT DID NOT MOVE.** The woman of about twenty-seven who washes that board, the man of about thirty-eight at the salt wharf, the man of about thirty-nine who trades on a board, the man of about sixty with the handcart, the woman of about sixty-nine and her tin, the man of about thirty-four with the chair, the man of about fifty-seven and his rope — which is in none of the ten. **No person was created, no descriptor was taken, nobody got stronger, nobody was thanked, nobody was told he was right, nobody was aged, and no chapter of these ten gave anybody a line they did not have.** The full figures after the repair are at `state/batch-summaries/volume-11-batch-0003.md` §7.6 and at the foot of `state/current.md`, with every first edition that moved published beside it.

# VOLUME 11, BATCH 0004 — Chapters 0531 to 0540, days 531 to 540

**This block is at the foot of the file and it wins over every block above it. No line above this one was edited. No person was created, no descriptor was taken, and nobody in these ten days got stronger, older, named, thanked or told he was right.**

**1. ADRIAN VALE. IN ONE OF THESE TEN AND IN NINE HE IS NOT NAMED AT ALL.** In 0535 alone, on the two hundred and twentieth day of the Bare Month, his hands are on nineteen boards off a stack and on the fifth one of them, and the want is for that row to come out even, and it came out with a gap in it at the fifth, and he put the fifth into the gap, and the row was even and the fifth was short. **HE IS THE CAUSE OF A MAN WITH A BARROW HAVING TO STOP IN THE MIDDLE OF THAT ROW, LIFT NINETEEN BOARDS IN TWO ARMS AND STAND THEM UP ON END AGAINST A WALL FOUR PACES ALONG, AND THE MAN DID NOT ASK WHOSE THEY WERE AND WAS NOT TOLD AND WAS NOT THANKED, AND THAT IS NOT A DECISION AND NOT A FIGURE OF THIS CITY AND NOT A DOCUMENT AND NOT A NOTICE AND NOT THE PANEL. NOBODY IN THAT MARKET KNEW HIS HANDS HAD BEEN ON THAT STACK AT ALL.** He has no quoted word in it, he reads no page in it, he is aged nowhere in it, nobody speaks to him in it, nobody in that chapter is waiting for him to be useful and he is not useful to one person in it. He was six rows up with his back to that row for the rest of that morning and he did not go back and look at the nineteen boards again. **HE IS NOT ADRIAN VALE IN ANY OF THE OTHER NINE AND NO CHAPTER OF THE TEN NAMES HIM OUTSIDE 0535.** He is Stage 2 on all ten days, the other world is not named once in the ten files, and the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across the ten on both readings.

**2. THE WOMAN WHO WALKED A MARKET. NOT NAMED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS.** **She is in NONE of them.** Day 531 to day 540 are ten days and none of them has her walking, none of them has her at the near end of eleven miles of flats, none of them has her at that stack of boards, and nobody in any of these ten days has said her name because she has not got one, and nobody in any of these ten days has asked her anything. **THE FIGURE OF HERS WENT OUT OF THIS CITY ON DAY 538, WHICH IS ONE OF THE TEN, AND SHE WAS NOT IN THAT ROOM AND WAS NOT ASKED FOR IT AND WAS NOT TOLD IT HAD GONE, and the chapter that filled the one place under the heading says out loud that there is no place on that sheet for the name of the person who made that figure and that nobody in that room asked who made it. HER FOUR MORNINGS ARE 511, 515, 517 AND 521 AND THIS BATCH PRINTED NO FIFTH MORNING AND NAMED NO FIGURE OFF ANY OF THE FOUR, AND ON DAY 532 THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THE FIRST OF THEM AND THE LAST OF THEM WAS DESCRIBED IN A ROOM OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE AS ABOUT A FIFTH AND THE FOUR OF THEM WERE NOT PRINTED.**

**3. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER.** **SHE HAS ABOUT FOURTEEN MARKED OCCURRENCES ACROSS THESE TEN FILES AND SHE IS THE BOLDED OR NAMED VOICE IN EIGHT OF THEM.** On day 531 she named out loud what the second one-place form is for, before anybody had asked her, and said that the name of the thing it is a figure of is not on that sheet and is not coming out of her head, **and she is the person this flood keeps naming that sheet, and she named it four days after it was read out and again eight days after that and again eleven days after that.** On day 532 she put the working slate on the table with the four of them on it and then did not say one word about it for the rest of that morning. On day 536 she carried the first one-place form down off the shelf and back up onto it and said out loud that a road four hundred miles long is a measure of a distance and not a place anybody in this city has stood in, and she was thanked by nobody. On day 537 she took the register off its shelf, stood it open, had it read down, and put it back, and her hand went near that shelf exactly once. **ON DAY 538 SHE FILLED THE ONE PLACE UNDER THE SECOND FORM'S HEADING WITH A FIGURE AND WITH NOTHING ELSE, IN HER OWN HAND, AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT IT IS GOING TO BE A FIGURE OF THIS CITY AND NOTHING ELSE AND THAT THERE IS NO PLACE ON THAT SHEET FOR THE NAME OF THE PERSON WHO MADE IT, AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM THANKED HER AND NOBODY ASKED HER WHO MADE THE FIGURE.** On day 540 she folded that sheet twice along its creases with a bone and shut it on the table and did not look up when the wall said its one thing. **THE THIRD COLUMN IS STILL EMPTY FROM THE TOP OF THE PAGE TO THE BOTTOM ON ALL TEN OF THESE DAYS AND SHE HAS NEVER GIVEN A REASON OUT LOUD AND NOBODY IN THIS VOLUME HAS ASKED HER FOR ONE. THE MIDDLE COLUMN IS FULL AND NO FIGURE WENT INTO IT ON ANY OF THESE TEN DAYS AND SHE IS THE ONLY PERSON WHO TOOK IT OFF THAT SHELF AND SHE TOOK IT OFF ONCE A DAY ON TWO OF them.**

**4. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL, AND THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE WHO TRADES ON A BOARD. TWO PEOPLE, NOT MERGED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, AND ONE OF THEM ASKED THE OTHER A QUESTION.** On day 533 he carried a chair up the stair at the back of that room and set it against the wall and nobody sat in it and nobody asked him where it came from. On day 532 he said out loud that a second figure of the same kind of thing, standing above the first one, that nobody is going to be made to agree with it, has a name, **and that word is the one this volume turns on, and it is on that page and in no other page of this batch, and it is in no mouth but his on any of these ten days.** On day 534 he asked the other of the two men a question, for the first time either of them had ever asked the other anything, in about nine words, about the man and not about the board, and he said in advance that he would rather nobody thanked him, and nobody did. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE ANSWERED IT ONCE, IN FOUR WORDS, OUT LOUD, IN THE LOCAL LANGUAGE, AND HE DID NOT SAY IT AGAIN, AND NOBODY IMPROVED ON IT, NOBODY ASKED HIM TO SAY IT AGAIN IN ANOTHER WAY, AND NEITHER MAN HAS SAID ONE WORD TO THE OTHER ON ANY DAY SINCE.** On day 539 they were in the same room again and neither of them said one word to the other and nobody in that room asked either of them about it. **The space about two fingers wide on the face of that board is not filled in any of these ten chapters and nobody in any of these ten chapters asked him about it and the name is not printed in any of them.** His board is a thing carried in 0534 and he took it back down the stair under his arm.

**5. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO CARRIES THINGS FOR A LIVING.** In two of these ten and in eight he is named but carries nothing. On day 533 he was at the window with the satchel with the flap on the bench beside him and his hands on his knees and nobody asked him what was in it and he did not put his hands in it. On day 540 he came up that stair with the satchel, set it on the bench, and **refused this volume's one rule out loud in about four seconds in a handful of words, saying that the rule is about a form and not about him, that he has carried five notices, a return, a sheet on the four hundred and first day of this flood and a sheet on the four hundred and fifty-first day of this flood without opening one of them, that this is the fifth thing he has carried that he knows what is in, and that the rule does not say one word about the man who carries it and that is why it is going to be true. NOBODY IMPROVED ON IT, NOBODY THANKED HIM, AND NOBODY ARGUED WITH HIM, AND HE WAS NOT ASKED WHAT WAS IN THE SHEET. THE SATCHEL WAS STILL ON THAT BENCH AT THE END OF THAT MORNING WITH THE FLAP DOWN AND THE SHEET WAS SHUT ON A TABLE AND NOT IN IT.**

**6. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WHO WASHES THAT BOARD.** Named in two of these ten chapters, 0531 and 0535, and in neither of them asked anything, thanked for anything, or given a reason. **HE IS NOT IN CHAPTER 0540, AND THE CHAPTER SAYS SO ITSELF IN A PLAIN SENTENCE OF NARRATION, and the woman at the window in that chapter says out loud that the man who washed that board every morning is not in that room and that she is not going to stand there and speak for him. THE WALL ABOVE THAT BOARD IS NOT WASHED ON ANY OF THESE TEN DAYS AND NO SECOND PERSON TOUCHED IT ON ANY OF THEM.**

**7. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT AT THE SALT WHARF.** In one of these ten, 0535, with his barrow and his tally-board, and one marked turn about nineteen boards that were not lying along that wall at the sixth hour and a row he was not going to stand in the middle of a market working out the ownership of. **HIS OWN FIGURE IS ON NO PAGE AND IS PRINTED NOWHERE IN THIS BATCH AND HE SAID NOTHING ABOUT IT AND NOBODY ASKED HIM ABOUT IT.** He is not the man who came up a lane with a barrow of boards and the two men are not merged on any page.

**8. THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN, THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY, AND EVERYBODY ELSE.** The man of about fifty-seven is in 0533 with a length of new rope over the back of his chair, and **no hand in that chapter lifted it, nobody in that room said one word about it, nobody asked him where the fence is, he was not told anything about it, and he was not looking at it.** The man of about sixty is in 0535 at the near gate with the handcart, the coil of rope and the hazel mallet with its head bound in cord, and nobody in that row asked him anything at all and nobody has asked him why he put the fence where he put it and nobody is going to. The woman of about sixty-nine is in none of these ten and her tin is on no page of this batch. Nobody in these ten days got stronger, no person arrived by narration, no descriptor was taken, no notice was written, no room was summoned, and **the wall said nothing on nine of these ten days and said one thing on the tenth.**

---

# VOLUME 11, BATCH 0004 — REVIEW REPAIR PASS

**This block is at the foot of the file and it wins over every block above it. The block headed `VOLUME 11, BATCH 0004 — Chapters 0531 to 0540` stands above it unedited. No entry above was changed. The full account is `state/batch-summaries/volume-11-batch-0004.md` §7.**

**NO CHARACTER MOVED AND NO CHARACTER CHANGED, AND THE ONLY THING THAT HAPPENED TO ANY MOUTH WAS ONE SPAN CORRECTED.**

1. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL.** In 0531, 0532, 0533, 0534, 0537, 0538, 0539 and 0540 as before, and in none of them thanked, told he was right, or asked anything. **On day 532 he no longer says the four came off in the same week; he says they came off the same eleven miles inside ten days in the same weather by the same person.** The rest of that turn is word for word as it was, including the fifth of the difference, and it still goes into no other mouth in this volume.
2. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE WHO TRADES ON A BOARD.** Unmoved. **The four words he gave on day 534 were not touched by this pass and are not described here.** The nineteen rates and the space two fingers wide along the foot of the middle column of his board are still named and still not asked about, and he still says one word to nobody in 0536, 0537, 0538 and 0539.
3. **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER.** Unmoved. She still names the second form before anybody asks, still says out loud that there is no place on it for the person who made the figure, still fills the one place on day 538 with a figure and nothing else, and still says on day 540 that nobody in that room is going to be asked anything about the wall. **Her three recut paragraphs are three passages that four and five other files had been sharing word for word; what she says in them did not change and how her room is described did.** The descriptor count for her is eleven on the repaired text and fourteen on the first edition, and the three she lost were recut sentences that named a man named on either side of them.
4. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO CARRIES THINGS FOR A LIVING.** Unmoved in what he does. **His refusal on day 540 is recut in its syntax and not in its substance: still about four seconds, still a handful of words, still that it is a rule about a form and not about him, still the five notices, the return, the sheet on day 401 and the sheet on day 451 and none of them opened, still that the rule does not say one word about the man who carries it and that is why it is going to be true, and still nobody improving on him, thanking him or arguing with him.** The cut was thirty-four words he had been sharing verbatim with the man of about twenty-seven in Chapter 0490.
5. **ADRIAN VALE.** **Still Stage 2 on all ten days, still in 0535 and nowhere else, still with no quoted word, still causing a man with a barrow to stop and lift nineteen boards in two arms, and still not useful to one person in that chapter.** The repair pass did not put him in a second chapter and did not touch his chapter's substance.
6. **THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WHO WASHES THAT BOARD.** Unmoved and still not asked. **He is still named in 0531 and 0535 and in neither of them asked anything, and he is still not in that room on day 540.**
7. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT AT THE SALT WHARF, THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN, AND THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY.** All unmoved. The barrow man still stops, lifts all nineteen in two arms and stands them up against a wall four paces along; the man of about fifty-seven still has the rope over the back of his chair with no hand on it; the man of about sixty still has the handcart at the near gate and is still asked nothing.
8. **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE.** **Still on no page of days 531 to 540, still with her three questions from days 346, 396 and 445 unanswered, and still with no fourth asked.** This pass did not put her on a page.
9. **THE WOMAN WHO WALKED A MARKET.** **Still on no page of these ten days, and her figure still went into the one place on day 538 and out of this city on day 545 without her on it.** This pass did not put her on a page and did not name her.

**AND NO NEW PERSON WAS INTRODUCED, NO DESCRIPTOR WAS TAKEN, AND THE DESCRIPTOR TABLE STANDS AT EIGHT WITH FIFTY OCCURRENCES ON THE REPAIRED TEXT AND FIFTY-THREE ON THE FIRST EDITION, AND NOT ONE OF THE EIGHT IS NEW.**
